# Mean Flow Distillation: Robust and Stable Distillation for Flow Matching Models

**作者：** An Zhao; Shengyuan Zhang; Zhongjian Sun; Yixiang Zhou; Zejian Li; Ling Yang; Tianrun Chen; Lingyun Sun

Mean Flow Distillation:
Robust and Stable Distillation for Flow Matching Models

<span id="S001-02"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-02

**Original:**

An Zhao 1 Shengyuan Zhang 1 Zhongjian Sun 2 Yixiang Zhou 1 Zejian Li 2 Ling Yang3 Tianrun Chen 1
Lingyun Sun 4

**中文:**

**作者：** An Zhao¹、Shengyuan Zhang¹、Zhongjian Sun²、Yixiang Zhou¹、Zejian Li²、Ling Yang³、Tianrun Chen¹、Lingyun Sun⁴

<span id="S001-03"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-03

**Original:**

Abstract
Flow Matching models have demonstrated strong
performance across a wide range of generative
tasks. However, their reliance on ODE-based it-
erative sampling incurs substantial computational
overhead in inference, which limits their applica-
bility in real-time scenes. While distillation is a
promising solution, existing approaches largely
borrow from diffusion-based score matching, of-
ten failing to exploit the intrinsic geometric struc-
ture of flows and suffering from training instabil-
ity, high variance, and degraded generation qual-
ity. In this paper, we propose Mean Flow Distil-
lation (MFD), a novel distillation framework tai-
lored for flow matching models. We theoretically
demonstrate that MFD acts as a temporal low-pass
filter, effectively suppressing the high-frequency
optimization noise inherent in variational score
distillation (VSD) while ensuring global trajec-
tory consistency. We further prove the Mean
Flow Matching Theorem, establishing that match-
ing expected average velocities is sufficient for
strict distribution alignment. Empirically, on chal-
lenging tasks of high-dimensional manifolds in-
cluding 4D occupancy forecasting and text-to-
image generation, MFD achieves state-of-the-art
performance, enabling high-fidelity single-step
generation. The implementation is available at
https://github.com/happyw1nd/MFD.

**中文:**

## 摘要

Flow Matching 模型在广泛的生成任务中展现出强劲性能。然而，其依赖基于 ODE 的迭代采样，在推理时带来可观的计算开销，限制了其在实时场景中的可用性。蒸馏是一种有前景的解决方案，但现有方法大多沿用基于扩散的分数匹配，往往未能利用流固有的几何结构，并受训练不稳定、高方差与生成质量下降的困扰。本文提出均值流蒸馏（Mean Flow Distillation, MFD），一个为 Flow Matching 模型量身定制的新蒸馏框架。我们从理论上证明 MFD 充当时间低通滤波器，能有效抑制变分分数蒸馏（variational score distillation, VSD）中固有的高频优化噪声，同时保证全局轨迹一致性。我们进一步证明均值流匹配定理（Mean Flow Matching Theorem），确立“匹配期望平均速度足以实现严格分布对齐”。在包括 4D 占据预测与文本到图像生成在内的高维流形挑战性任务上，MFD 达到最先进性能，可实现高保真单步生成。代码已开源于 https://github.com/happyw1nd/MFD 。

<span id="S001-05"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-05

**Original:**

1. Introduction

**中文:**

## 1 引言

---

<span id="S001-06"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-06

**Original:**

In recent years, flow-based generative modeling based on
Flow Matching (FM) and stochastic interpolants (Liu et al.,
2023; Lipman et al., 2023; Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden,
2023) has emerged as a powerful paradigm, demonstrating
remarkable performance in applications like image gener-
ation (Qin et al., 2025b) and 3D modelling (Xiang et al.,
2025). Unlike score-based diffusion models (Yin et al.,
2024a; Wang et al., 2023), FM directly learns a vector field
connecting marginal distributions (Liu et al., 2023; Lipman
et al., 2023), offering a more explicit dynamical description
and superior generation quality. However, FM relies on
time-consuming iterative sampling (Luo et al., 2023; Lip-
man et al., 2024). As model scales grow, high inference
latency limits FM’s applicability in real-time or resource-
constrained scenarios (Zhou et al., 2025b).

**中文:**

近年来，基于 Flow Matching（FM）与随机插值（stochastic interpolants）的流式生成建模（Liu et al., 2023; Lipman et al., 2023; Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden, 2023）已成为一种强有力的范式，在图像生成（Qin et al., 2025b）与 3D 建模（Xiang et al., 2025）等应用中展现出卓越性能。与基于分数的扩散模型（Yin et al., 2024a; Wang et al., 2023）不同，FM 直接学习连接各边缘分布的向量场（Liu et al., 2023; Lipman et al., 2023），提供更显式的动力学描述与更优的生成质量。然而，FM 依赖耗时的迭代采样（Luo et al., 2023; Lipman et al., 2024）。随着模型规模增长，高推理延迟限制了 FM 在实时或资源受限场景中的适用性（Zhou et al., 2025b）。

> 文中引用采用 (作者, 年份) 形式，按原文照录。

---

<span id="S001-07"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-07

**Original:**

The existing acceleration methods, such as advanced sched-
ulers (Liu et al., 2022; Lu et al., 2022) or truncation (Zheng
et al., 2023), often fail to maintain quality in few-step
regimes. Distillation remains the most promising strat-
egy (Yin et al., 2024a; Luo et al., 2023), yet existing ap-
proaches are primarily designed for score-based formula-
tions. Directly adapting these to FM often involves indirect
score conversions (Zhou et al., 2025b), failing to exploit
FM’s intrinsic geometric structures. This mismatch typ-
ically leads to training instability, high variance, and de-
graded generation quality (Ge et al., 2026).

**中文:**

现有的加速方法，如先进调度器（Liu et al., 2022; Lu et al., 2022）或截断（Zheng et al., 2023），在少步情形下往往难以保持质量。蒸馏仍是最有前景的策略（Yin et al., 2024a; Luo et al., 2023），但现有方法主要是为基于分数的表述而设计的。把它们直接迁移到 FM 通常需要间接的分数转换（Zhou et al., 2025b），无法利用 FM 固有的几何结构。这种不匹配通常导致训练不稳定、高方差与生成质量下降（Ge et al., 2026）。

---

<span id="S001-08"></span>
**Source:** p.1 S001-08

**Original:**

To address these challenges, we proposeMean Flow Dis-
tillation (MFD), a general-purpose framework tailored for
pretrained FM models. Unlike methods that forcibly match
instantaneous velocity fields or rely on high-variance score
estimates (Ge et al., 2026; Yang et al., 2025), MFD utilizes
Mean Flow, the time-integrated velocity field, as the core
alignment metric. Our key insight is that the cumulative
displacement induced by the mean flow provides a stable,
global consistency constraint, effectively filtering out local
geometric perturbations common in instantaneous matching.
Technically, MFD alternates between a learnable Auxil-
iary Flow Model, which approximates the student-induced
vector field, and the student, which matches the auxiliary

**中文:**

为应对这些挑战，我们提出均值流蒸馏（MFD），一个面向预训练 FM 模型的通用框架。与强行匹配瞬时速度场或依赖高方差分数估计的方法（Ge et al., 2026; Yang et al., 2025）不同，MFD 以均值流（Mean Flow）——即时间积分的速度场——作为核心对齐度量。我们的关键洞见是：由均值流诱导的累积位移提供了一个稳定、全局的一致性约束，能有效滤除瞬时匹配中常见的局部几何扰动。在技术上，MFD 在可学习的辅助流模型（Auxiliary Flow Model）与学生模型之间交替：前者近似学生诱导的向量场，后者把

## PDF 第 2 页

<span id="S002-C1"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-C1

**Original:**

Figure 1.Overview of Mean Flow Distillation (MFD).(1)Sample noise X0 ∼ N(0, I) , generate X1 via the single-step student model
Gθ, and construct intermediate state Xs through linear interpolation following Eq (2) with t replaced by s.(2)Compute the auxiliary
mean flow by integrating the auxiliary model vP from Xs over interval [s, t] via ODE solver Φ following Eq (16).(3)Compute the
teacher mean flow by integrating the pretrained teacher model uQ from the same Xs over [s, t] also following Eq (16).(4)Optimize
the student model Gθ by matching the two mean flows, with gradients backpropagated through Xs following Eq (20).(5)Update the
auxiliary model vP to track the evolving student distribution P following Eq (3). This temporal integration over [s, t] provides more
robust and low-variance gradients compared to instantaneous velocity matching, enabling stable distillation with high-quality generation.

**中文:**

**图 1：均值流蒸馏（MFD）概览。** (1) 采样噪声 $X_0 \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$，通过单步学生模型 $G_\theta$ 生成 $X_1$，并按式 (2)（其中 $t$ 替换为 $s$）经线性插值构造中间状态 $X_s$。(2) 用 ODE 求解器 $\Phi$ 按式 (16) 从 $X_s$ 出发在区间 $[s, t]$ 上积分辅助模型 $v_P$，计算辅助均值流。(3) 同样按式 (16)，从同一 $X_s$ 出发在 $[s, t]$ 上积分预训练教师模型 $u_Q$，计算教师均值流。(4) 通过匹配两个均值流来优化学生模型 $G_\theta$，梯度按式 (20) 经 $X_s$ 反向传播。(5) 按式 (3) 更新辅助模型 $v_P$ 以跟踪演化中的学生分布 $P$。相比瞬时速度匹配，这种在 $[s, t]$ 上的时间积分提供了更鲁棒、更低方差的梯度，从而实现稳定蒸馏与高质量生成。

**Original:**

model’s average velocity to that of a frozen teacher via multi-
step ODE integration. The auxiliary model is used only
during training and can be implemented with lightweight

MFD acts as a temporal low-pass filter that suppresses high-

and show that MFD yields lower variance than variational

**中文:**

模型的平均速度与冻结教师模型经多步 ODE 积分得到的平均速度对齐（承接第 1 页末句）。辅助模型仅在训练时使用，在大规模设置中可用轻量适配器实现（第 5.3 节）。在理论上，MFD 充当时间低通滤波器，抑制高频优化噪声。我们建立均值流匹配定理，证明匹配期望平均速度足以实现严格的分布对齐，并表明 MFD 的方差低于变分分数蒸馏（VSD）（Wang et al., 2023）。

Our contributions are summarized as follows:

我们的贡献总结如下：

---

<span id="S002-04"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-04

**Original:**

• We propose Mean Flow Distillation (MFD), the first
distillation paradigm that directly aligns velocity fields
for Flow Matching models, avoiding indirect score
conversion. By using the time-integrated mean flow as
the supervision signal, MFD enables stable and high-
quality distillation.

**中文:**

- 我们提出均值流蒸馏（MFD），这是首个直接为 Flow Matching 模型对齐速度场的蒸馏范式，避免了间接的分数转换。通过使用时间积分的均值流作为监督信号，MFD 实现了稳定且高质量的蒸馏。

> 贡献列表第 1 条。

---

<span id="S002-05"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-05

**Original:**

• We formalize Instantaneous Velocity Field Matching
and prove the Mean Flow Matching Theorem, showing
that matching the mean flow of models will achieve
strict distributional alignment. We further theoretically
analyze the gradient variance and demonstrate that
MFD yields lower variance and more stable training
dynamics than variational score distillation.

**中文:**

- 我们形式化了瞬时速度场匹配（Instantaneous Velocity Field Matching），并证明均值流匹配定理，表明匹配模型的均值流可实现严格的分布对齐。我们进一步从理论上分析梯度方差，证明相比变分分数蒸馏，MFD 具有更低的方差与更稳定的训练动态。

> 贡献列表第 2 条。

---

<span id="S002-06"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-06

**Original:**

• On two challenging tasks, text-to-image generation
and 4D occupancy forecasting, MFD outperforms ex-
isting distillation methods, including score-based and
consistency-based flow distillation, in generation qual-
ity and distributional fidelity.

**中文:**

- 在文本到图像生成与 4D 占据预测两个挑战性任务上，MFD 在生成质量与分布保真度上优于现有蒸馏方法，包括基于分数的与基于一致性的流蒸馏方法。

2. Related Works

**中文:**

## 2 相关工作

---

<span id="S002-09"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-09

**Original:**

2.1. Flow Matching

**中文:**

### 2.1 Flow Matching

---

<span id="S002-10"></span>
**Source:** p.2 S002-10

**Original:**

Lipman et al. (2023) introduced Flow Matching (FM)
to train continuous normalizing flows by regressing ve-
locity fields along probability paths, while stochastic in-
terpolants (Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden, 2023) indepen-
dently developed a closely related continuous-time con-
struction based on interpolating paths. Concurrently, Recti-
fied Flow (Liu et al., 2023) emphasized straightening trans-
port trajectories to approach geodesics, significantly reduc-
ing integration error for few-step sampling. FM has since
matured into a general-purpose paradigm applied to text-
to-image (Qin et al., 2025a; Xie et al., 2025), 3D genera-
tion (Xiang et al., 2025; Chen et al., 2025) and 4D occupancy
forecasting (Liu et al., 2025).

**中文:**

Lipman et al. (2023) 提出 Flow Matching（FM），通过沿概率路径回归速度场来训练连续归一化流；而随机插值（Albergo & Vanden-Eijnden, 2023）独立地基于插值路径发展出密切相关的连续时间构造。同时，整流流（Rectified Flow）（Liu et al., 2023）强调把传输轨迹拉直以逼近测地线，显著降低少步采样的积分误差。此后 FM 已发展为通用范式，被应用于文本到图像（Qin et al., 2025a; Xie et al., 2025）、3D 生成（Xiang et al., 2025; Chen et al., 2025）与 4D 占据预测（Liu et al., 2025）。

<span id="page-3"></span>
## PDF 第 3 页

<span id="S003-01"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-01

**Original:**

Recent research targets one-step generation within this
framework. Mean Flow (MF) (Geng et al., 2025a) replaces
instantaneous velocity supervision with time-integrated
mean velocity, achieving one-step generation without dis-
tillation. Improved Mean Flow (Geng et al., 2025b) further
refines this by addressing target-network dependencies. In-
ductive Moment Matching (Zhou et al., 2025a) proposes
a single-stage training procedure for few-step generation,
guaranteeing distribution-level convergence without requir-
ing pre-trained initialization. However, these methods are
designed for end-to-end training from scratch. Their ob-
jectives cannot be directly applied to distill existing high-
quality pretrained models, limiting their utility as general-
purpose acceleration mechanisms. We provide a detailed
comparison between MFD, MF, and related flow distillation
methods in Section B.1.

**中文:**

该框架内的近期研究聚焦于单步生成。均值流（Mean Flow, MF）（Geng et al., 2025a）用时间积分的平均速度替代瞬时速度监督，无需蒸馏即可实现单步生成。改进的均值流（Improved Mean Flow）（Geng et al., 2025b）通过处理目标网络依赖进一步改进这一点。归纳矩匹配（Inductive Moment Matching）（Zhou et al., 2025a）提出一种用于少步生成的单阶段训练流程，保证分布级收敛而无需预训练初始化。然而，这些方法是为从零开始的端到端训练而设计的，其目标无法直接用于蒸馏已有的高质量预训练模型，限制了它们作为通用加速机制的用途。我们在第 B.1 节给出 MFD、MF 与相关流蒸馏方法的详细比较。

> 承接 S002-10 所在的 2.1 节。

---

<span id="S003-02"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-02

**Original:**

2.2. Sampling Acceleration via Distillation

**中文:**

### 2.2 通过蒸馏实现采样加速

---

<span id="S003-03"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-03

**Original:**

Distillation methods aim to compress the computationally
expensive iterative sampling process into a few steps or even
a single step. These approaches generally fall into two main
categories: Consistency Distillation and Score Distillation.
The principle of consistency distillation is to enforce trajec-
tory self-consistency, ensuring that points along the prob-
ability flow ODE consistently map to corresponding ini-
tial states. Progressive Distillation (Salimans & Ho, 2022)
achieves acceleration by iteratively halving the number of
sampling steps. Consistency Models (Song et al., 2023)
propose learning a mapping from any timestep to the data
origin. Latent Consistency Models (LCM) (Luo et al., 2024)
apply this paradigm to latent space, while Multistep Con-
sistency Models (MCM) (Heek et al., 2024) introduce a
generalized formulation that bridges the gap between single-
step and multi-step generation regimes. Align Your Flow
(AYF) (Sabour et al., 2025) introduces continuous-time flow
map distillation, generalizing consistency models to connect
arbitrary noise while maintaining effectiveness across all
steps. AYF enforces sample-level flow-map consistency,
whereas MFD matches distribution-level expected average
velocity fields; this distinction is analyzed in Section B.1.

**中文:**

蒸馏方法旨在把计算昂贵的迭代采样过程压缩为少数几步甚至单步。这些方法大体分为两类：一致性蒸馏与分数蒸馏。一致性蒸馏的原理是强制轨迹自一致性，确保沿概率流 ODE 的点一致地映射到相应的初始状态。渐进蒸馏（Progressive Distillation）（Salimans & Ho, 2022）通过反复把采样步数减半来实现加速。一致性模型（Consistency Models）（Song et al., 2023）提出学习从任意时间步到数据原点的映射。隐一致性模型（Latent Consistency Models, LCM）（Luo et al., 2024）把该范式应用于隐空间，而多步一致性模型（Multistep Consistency Models, MCM）（Heek et al., 2024）引入一种广义表述，弥合单步与多步生成区间之间的差距。Align Your Flow（AYF）（Sabour et al., 2025）引入连续时间流映射蒸馏，把一致性模型推广到连接任意噪声，并在所有步数上保持有效性。AYF 强制样本级流映射一致性，而 MFD 匹配分布级的期望平均速度场；这一区别在第 B.1 节分析。

---

<span id="S003-04"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-04

**Original:**

Instead of relying on trajectory-level constraints, score dis-
tillation optimizes the student generator by aligning its gra-
dients with the density fields implied by the teacher model.
Variational Score Distillation (VSD) (Wang et al., 2023)
utilizes the teacher as a score-based prior to guide the stu-
dent’s learning. Techniques such as Diff-Instruct (Luo et al.,
2023), DMD (Yin et al., 2024b), and DMD2 (Yin et al.,
2024a) explicitly minimize the KL divergence between the
student and teacher distributions through score matching
objectives. More recent advances (Sauer et al., 2024) further
enhance generation quality by combining score distillation
with adversarial training objectives. Additionally, Score
Identity Distillation (SiD) (Zhou et al., 2024) accelerates
the distillation process by leveraging novel score identities
that eliminate the need for real data components. Swift-
Brush (Nguyen & Tran, 2024) demonstrates the feasibility
of image-free distillation using variational score distillation.

**中文:**

分数蒸馏不依赖轨迹级约束，而是通过把学生生成器的梯度与教师模型所隐含的密度场对齐来优化学生。变分分数蒸馏（VSD）（Wang et al., 2023）利用教师作为基于分数的先验来指导学生学习。Diff-Instruct（Luo et al., 2023）、DMD（Yin et al., 2024b）与 DMD2（Yin et al., 2024a）等技术通过分数匹配目标显式最小化学生与教师分布之间的 KL 散度。更近的进展（Sauer et al., 2024）通过把分数蒸馏与对抗训练目标结合起来，进一步提升生成质量。此外，分数恒等蒸馏（Score Identity Distillation, SiD）（Zhou et al., 2024）利用新的分数恒等式加快蒸馏过程，从而免去对真实数据成分的需求。SwiftBrush（Nguyen & Tran, 2024）展示了使用变分分数蒸馏进行无图蒸馏的可行性。

---

<span id="S003-05"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-05

**Original:**

Recently, several works apply these techniques to flow
matching models (Liu et al., 2024; Ge et al., 2026). How-
ever, these methods typically necessitate converting velocity
fields into score functions (Zhou et al., 2025b). This con-
version is often suboptimal, as it fails to fully exploit the
deterministic ODE structures intrinsic to flow matching.

**中文:**

最近有若干工作把这些技术应用于 Flow Matching 模型（Liu et al., 2024; Ge et al., 2026）。然而，这些方法通常需要把速度场转换为分数函数（Zhou et al., 2025b）。这种转换往往次优，因为它未能充分利用 Flow Matching 固有的确定性 ODE 结构。

---

3. Preliminaries

**中文:**

## 3 预备知识

---

<span id="S003-07"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-07

**Original:**

3.1. Flow Matching

**中文:**

### 3.1 Flow Matching

---

<span id="S003-08"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-08

**Original:**

Flow Matching is a class of methods that perform distribu-
tion transformation by learning ordinary differential equa-
tions (ODEs). The objective is to directly learn a velocity
field that maps an initial distribution π0 to a target distribu-
tion π1, without relying on stochastic diffusion processes.
Rectified Flow provides a simple yet effective instantiation
of flow matching by directly regressing toward straight-
line interpolation paths between noise and data, yielding a
straightforward MSE training objective.

**中文:**

Flow Matching 是一类通过学习常微分方程（ODE）来完成分布变换的方法。其目标是直接学习一个把初始分布 $\pi_0$ 映射到目标分布 $\pi_1$ 的速度场，而不依赖随机扩散过程。整流流通过直接回归噪声与数据之间的直线插值路径，为 Flow Matching 提供了一个简单而有效的实例，从而得到直接的 MSE 训练目标。

---

<span id="S003-09"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-09

**Original:**

Consider two probability distributions π0 and π1 in Rd.
Flow matching tries to learn an ODE that defines a deter-
ministic transport process mapping samples fromπ 0 toπ 1

**中文:**

考虑 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中的两个概率分布 $\pi_0$ 与 $\pi_1$。Flow Matching 试图学习一个 ODE，它定义把 $\pi_0$ 的样本映射到 $\pi_1$ 的确定性传输过程

---

**Source:** p.3 S003-10

dt =v(Z t, t), t∈[0,1],(1)

---

<span id="S003-11"></span>
**Source:** p.3 S003-11

**Original:**

The velocity field v:R d ×[0,1]→R d is learned in a
data-driven manner and satisfiesZ 0 ∼π 0 andZ 1 ∼π 1.

**中文:**

其中速度场 $v: \mathbb{R}^d \times [0,1] \to \mathbb{R}^d$ 以数据驱动方式学习，并满足 $Z_0 \sim \pi_0$ 与 $Z_1 \sim \pi_1$。

---

The core idea of flow matching is to adopt a simple interpo-
lation process as a reference trajectory and train a model to
learn the corresponding dynamics to match this trajectory.
Specifically, a linear interpolation betweenX 0 andX 1 is:

**中文:**

Flow Matching 的核心思想是采用一个简单的插值过程作为参考轨迹，并训练模型学习相应的动力学以匹配该轨迹。具体地，$X_0$ 与 $X_1$ 之间的线性插值为：

Xt = (1−t)X 0 +tX 1,(2)

**Original:**

The time derivative is constant, given by ˙Xt =X 1 −X 0.
Rather than directly using this non-causal path, which de-
pends on future information, rectified flow learns a velocity
field v(Xt, t) that approximates the instantaneous veloc-
ity of the interpolation trajectory by minimizing a mean
squared error (MSE) objective. Concretely, the velocity
field is learned by minimizing the following least-squares
objective:

**中文:**

其时间导数为常数 $\dot{X}_t = X_1 - X_0$。整流流并不直接使用这种依赖未来信息的非因果路径，而是学习一个速度场 $v(X_t, t)$，通过最小化均方误差（MSE）目标来逼近插值轨迹的瞬时速度。具体地，速度场通过最小化如下最小二乘目标来学习：

[第 3 页](#page-3)

<span id="page-4"></span>
## PDF 第 4 页

<span id="S004-01"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S004-01

**Original:**

That is, conditioned on a given position X and time t, the
velocity field equals the expected direction of all linear paths
passing through that point. The resulting velocity field de-
pends only on the current state and time, thereby defining a
well-posed ODE that can be simulated in a forward manner.
The trajectory Zt generated under this velocity field en-
joys an important marginal preservation property: for any
t∈[0,1] , the random variable Zt follows the same distribu-
tion as the interpolationXt. Thus, the terminal state satisfies
Z1 ∼π 1. Moreover, due to the uniqueness of ODE solu-
tions, different trajectories do not intersect during evolution.
This non-crossing property consolidates potentially entan-
gled linear interpolation paths into a coherent deterministic
flow, giving rise to the rectified flow (Liu et al., 2023).

**中文:**

也就是说，在给定位置 $X$ 与时间 $t$ 的条件下，速度场等于所有经过该点的线性路径的期望方向。所得速度场只依赖当前状态与时间，从而定义了一个可以前向模拟的适定 ODE。在该速度场下生成的轨迹 $Z_t$ 具有重要的边缘保持性质：对任意 $t \in [0,1]$，随机变量 $Z_t$ 与插值 $X_t$ 服从相同的分布。因此终端状态满足 $Z_1 \sim \pi_1$。此外，由于 ODE 解的唯一性，不同轨迹在演化过程中不会相交。这种非交叉性质把可能相互纠缠的线性插值路径整合成一个连贯的确定性流，从而产生整流流（Liu et al., 2023）。

Score distillation is inspired by VSD (Wang et al., 2023),
which aims to directly distill a multi-step teacher diffusion
model into a few-step or single-step student model. Specifi-
cally, let q be the ground truth distribution approximated by
the pre-trained teacher model uQ, p1 be the generative dis-
tribution of the student model Gθ, where θ is the trainable
parameter. Score distillation minimizes the KL divergence
betweenqandp 1 (Wang et al., 2023; Yin et al., 2024a)

**中文:**

分数蒸馏受 VSD（Wang et al., 2023）启发，其目标是把多步教师扩散模型直接蒸馏为少步或单步学生模型。具体地，设 $q$ 为预训练教师模型 $u_Q$ 所近似的真实分布，$p_1$ 为学生模型 $G_\theta$ 的生成分布，其中 $\theta$ 为可训练参数。分数蒸馏最小化 $q$ 与 $p_1$ 之间的 KL 散度（Wang et al., 2023; Yin et al., 2024a）

**Original:**

During training, the student model and the auxiliary model
are optimized alternately, with the optimization schedule

训练过程中，学生模型与辅助模型交替优化，优化调度可根据具体任务需求配置。

<span id="S004-14"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S004-14

**Original:**

Recent works apply score distillation to flow matching mod-
els (Liu et al., 2024; Ge et al., 2026). Specifically, they
convert the velocity field v(Xt, t) to a score function via
s(Xt, t)≈ Xt−v(Xt,t)
(1−t)2 (Liu et al., 2024). Such conversion
is suboptimal for two reasons. First, division by(1−t) 2 am-
plifies velocity-estimation errors as t approaches 1, directly
increasing gradient variance. Second, the converted score
is matched at independently sampled timesteps, discarding
the deterministic ODE trajectory structure that FM models
explicitly learn. MFD therefore operates directly in velocity
space and integrates supervision along flow trajectories.

**中文:**

近期工作把分数蒸馏应用于 Flow Matching 模型（Liu et al., 2024; Ge et al., 2026）。具体地，它们通过 $s(X_t, t) \approx \frac{X_t - v(X_t,t)}{(1-t)^2}$ 把速度场 $v(X_t, t)$ 转换为分数函数（Liu et al., 2024）。这种转换在两方面是次优的。第一，除以 $(1-t)^2$ 会在 $t$ 趋近 1 时放大速度估计误差，直接增大梯度方差。第二，转换后的分数是在独立采样的时间步上做匹配的，丢弃了 FM 模型显式学到的确定性 ODE 轨迹结构。因此 MFD 直接在速度空间中操作，并沿流轨迹积分监督信号。

---

<span id="S004-15"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S004-15

**Original:**

4. Mean Flow Distillation

**中文:**

## 4 均值流蒸馏

---

<span id="S004-16"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S004-16

**Original:**

Existing distillation methods for flow matching models typ-
ically convert the velocity field into an equivalent score
function and then apply score-based methods developed
for diffusion models (Zhou et al., 2025b; Ge et al., 2026).
While this inherits score matching, it overlooks the intrin-
sic structure of flow matching (the deterministic ODE tra-
jectories and explicit velocity field formulation). In flow
matching, a velocity field v(x, t) uniquely defines a deter-
ministic transport path from a source distribution to a target
distribution, implying thattwo flows with identical velocity
fields generate identical trajectories and distributions.

**中文:**

面向 Flow Matching 模型的现有蒸馏方法通常把速度场转换为等价的分数函数，然后套用为扩散模型开发的基于分数的方法（Zhou et al., 2025b; Ge et al., 2026）。这虽然继承了分数匹配，却忽略了 Flow Matching 的内在结构（确定性 ODE 轨迹与显式速度场表述）。在 Flow Matching 中，速度场 $v(x,t)$ 唯一地定义了从源分布到目标分布的确定性传输路径，这意味着具有相同速度场的两个流会生成完全相同的轨迹与分布。

---

<span id="S004-17"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S004-17

**Original:**

This observation motivates an intuitive distillation paradigm
that directly aligns velocity fields, rather than relying on an
indirect score transformation.

**中文:**

这一观察启发了一个直观的蒸馏范式：直接对齐速度场，而不是依赖间接的分数变换。

---

<span id="S004-18"></span>
**Source:** p.4 S004-18

**Original:**

We formalize this idea via Instantaneous Velocity Field
Matching theorem (Theorem 4.1), which provides a the-
oretical basis for direct velocity-based distillation. How-
ever, instantaneous matching uses supervision from a single
sampled timestep. Empirically, VSD-style instantaneous
baselines show wider loss distributions and more oscillatory
training curves (Figure 2 and Section D.5); theoretically,
their gradient variance is tied to the noise of a single instan-
taneous estimate (Section C.3). To address this, inspired by
mean flow theory (Geng et al., 2025a), we propose Mean
Flow Distillation (Theorem 4.3), which replaces pointwise
velocity alignment with matching time-integrated velocity
fields, yielding stable and effective distillation.

**中文:**

我们通过瞬时速度场匹配定理（定理 4.1）将该想法形式化，它为直接基于速度的蒸馏提供了理论基础。然而，瞬时匹配只使用单个采样时间步的监督。在经验上，VSD 式瞬时基线表现出更宽的损失分布与更振荡的训练曲线（图 2 与第 D.5 节）；在理论上，它们的梯度方差与单次瞬时估计的噪声相关（第 C.3 节）。为此，受均值流理论（Geng et al., 2025a）启发，我们提出均值流蒸馏（定理 4.3），用匹配时间积分的速度场替代逐点速度对齐，从而获得稳定而有效的蒸馏。

---

[第 4 页](#page-4)

## PDF 第 5 页

<span id="S005-01"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-01

**Original:**

4.1. Problem Setup

**中文:**

### 4.1 问题设置

---

<span id="S005-02"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-02

**Original:**

Let p0 be the source distribution, set as N(0, I) by default
in a space Rd, and Q be the target distribution of the training
samples. We consider three key entities:

**中文:**

设 $p_0$ 为源分布，默认为 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中的 $\mathcal{N}(0, I)$，$Q$ 为训练样本的目标分布。我们考虑三个关键实体：

---

<span id="S005-03"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-03

**Original:**

Theteacher modelis a pretrained flow matching model
uQ :R d ×[0,1]→R d, which defines a probability flow
transporting samples from p0 to Q. The corresponding flow
map is denoted as ψQ
t , specified by the time-dependent
instantaneous velocity field of the pretrained distribution Q.

**中文:**

**教师模型** 是一个预训练的 Flow Matching 模型 $u_Q: \mathbb{R}^d \times [0,1] \to \mathbb{R}^d$，它定义了一个把样本从 $p_0$ 传输到 $Q$ 的概率流。相应的流映射记为 $\psi_t^Q$，由预训练分布 $Q$ 的时变瞬时速度场确定。

---

<span id="S005-04"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-04

**Original:**

Thestudent model Gθ :R d →R d is a one-step generator
with trainable parameters denoted as θ. Gθ maps a latent
noise X0 ∼p 0 to a generated sample X1 =G θ(X0). The
marginal distribution of generated samples is defined as P .
Both P and Q are defined on Rd and share the same source
p0. Intuitively, the training objective is to optimize θ such
thatPclosely matches the targetQ.

**中文:**

**学生模型** $G_\theta: \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}^d$ 是一个单步生成器，其可训练参数记为 $\theta$。$G_\theta$ 把隐噪声 $X_0 \sim p_0$ 映射为生成样本 $X_1 = G_\theta(X_0)$。生成样本的边缘分布定义为 $P$。$P$ 与 $Q$ 都定义在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上并共享同一个源 $p_0$。直观上，训练目标是优化 $\theta$ 使 $P$ 与目标 $Q$ 紧密匹配。

---

<span id="S005-05"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-05

**Original:**

Since the student model is a single-step mapping and does
not explicitly provide probability flow information, anaux-
iliary flow model vP :R d ×[0,1]→R d is introduced
to approximate the student distribution P . Without loss of
generality, we assume that vP can exactly reconstruct the
instantaneous velocity field of the distribution P , with the
corresponding flow map ψP
t satisfying ψP
1 (X0)∼P . The
use of an auxiliary flow model is both necessary and theoret-
ically justified; see Section C.4 for the proof and Section 5.3
for its capacity and overhead in practice. The overview of
Mean Flow Distillation is shown in Figure 1.

**中文:**

由于学生模型是单步映射，不显式提供概率流信息，因此引入**辅助流模型** $v_P: \mathbb{R}^d \times [0,1] \to \mathbb{R}^d$ 来近似学生分布 $P$。不失一般性，我们假设 $v_P$ 能精确重建分布 $P$ 的瞬时速度场，相应的流映射 $\psi_t^P$ 满足 $\psi_1^P(X_0) \sim P$。辅助流模型的使用既是必要的，也在理论上有依据；证明见第 C.4 节，其在实际中的容量与开销见第 5.3 节。均值流蒸馏的概览见图 1。

---

<span id="S005-06"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-06

**Original:**

4.2. Instantaneous Velocity Field Matching

**中文:**

### 4.2 瞬时速度场匹配

---

<span id="S005-07"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-07

**Original:**

Building on the trajectory uniqueness property of flow
matching, we first establish a theoretical foundation for
flow-based distillation through instantaneous velocity field
alignment. Theorem 4.1 shows that matching velocity fields
pointwise is sufficient to ensure distribution matching.

**中文:**

基于 Flow Matching 的轨迹唯一性，我们首先通过瞬时速度场对齐为基于流的蒸馏建立理论基础。定理 4.1 表明，逐点匹配速度场足以保证分布匹配。

---

uQ and vP be the instantaneous velocity fields correspond-

(D(x, y) = 0⇐ ⇒x=y ). If the expected Bregman
divergence between the instantaneous velocity fields is zero:

Then the student distribution coincides with the teacher

---

<span id="S005-11"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-11

**Original:**

The proof of Theorem 4.1 is shown in Section C.1.

**中文:**

定理 4.1 的证明见第 C.1 节。

---

<span id="S005-12"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-12

**Original:**

While instantaneous velocity matching provides a theoret-
ically sound foundation for distillation, it can be further
improved in practice. Due to its reliance on pointwise veloc-
ity alignment at individual time steps, this approach exhibits
sensitivity to local perturbations in Xt and does not explic-
itly leverage the cumulative trajectory information over time
intervals. These observations motivate us to develop a more
robust alignment criterion that better captures the global
structure of flows through temporal integration. As in Sec-
tion C.3, when reduced to single-step integration, our pro-
posed method (MFD) degenerates to Instantaneous Velocity
Field Matching with comparable variance and training sta-
bility to VSD, confirming that the benefits arise specifically
from multi-step temporal averaging.

**中文:**

虽然瞬时速度匹配为蒸馏提供了理论上可靠的基础，但它在实践中仍可改进。由于它依赖单个时间步上的逐点速度对齐，该方式对 $X_t$ 的局部扰动敏感，且未显式利用跨时间区间的累积轨迹信息。这些观察促使我们发展一种更鲁棒的对齐准则，通过时间积分更好地刻画流的全局结构。如第 C.3 节所示，当退化为单步积分时，我们所提方法（MFD）退化为瞬时速度场匹配，其方差与训练稳定性与 VSD 相当，这证实其收益恰好来源于多步时间平均。

---

<span id="S005-13"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-13

**Original:**

4.3. Mean Velocity Field Matching

**中文:**

### 4.3 平均速度场匹配

---

<span id="S005-14"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-14

**Original:**

Recent work on Mean Flows for one-step generative model-
ing (Geng et al., 2025a) demonstrates that supervising with
time-integrated mean velocities rather than instantaneous
velocities leads to better generation quality. We apply this
idea to the distillation setting with a key insight: temporal
integration acts as a low-pass filter, effectively averaging
out high-frequency noise in instantaneous velocity estimates
and providing more robust, low-variance gradients for stu-
dent model optimization.

**中文:**

关于用于单步生成建模的均值流的近期工作（Geng et al., 2025a）表明，用时间积分的平均速度而非瞬时速度进行监督会带来更好的生成质量。我们把这一想法应用于蒸馏设置，并给出一个关键洞见：时间积分充当低通滤波器，有效平均掉瞬时速度估计中的高频噪声，为学生模型优化提供更鲁棒、低方差的梯度。

---

<span id="S005-15"></span>
**Source:** p.5 S005-15

**Original:**

Compared to pointwise instantaneous matching, matching
time-integrated mean velocities offers stronger supervision
signals that naturally capture global trajectory consistency
rather than volatile local tangent alignment. While one
intuitive perspective suggests that velocity errors may accu-
mulate during integration over [s, t] to amplify supervision
signals, we discuss this viewpoint and its relationship to the
variance reduction mechanism in Section B.5.

**中文:**

与逐点瞬时匹配相比，匹配时间积分的平均速度提供了更强的监督信号，能自然捕获全局轨迹一致性，而非易变的局部切向对齐。虽然一种直观看法认为速度误差会在 $[s,t]$ 上积分时累积从而放大监督信号，我们在第 B.5 节讨论该观点及其与方差降低机制的关系。

---

**Source:** p.5 S005-16

tory from timesto timet:

[第 5 页](#page-5)

<span id="page-6"></span>
## PDF 第 6 页

<span id="S006-01"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-01

**Original:**

A VF acts as a temporal low-pass filter on the instantaneous
velocity field (detailed analysis in Section B.3). By integrat-
ing over time, it filters out high-frequency stochastic noise
and optimization artifacts inherent in gradient estimation
(e.g., mini-batch sampling noise, numerical instabilities) and
this provides a smoother supervision signal. This motivates
our main theoretical result, which shows that matching aver-
age velocity fields is sufficient for distribution alignment.

**中文:**

AVF 充当瞬时速度场的时间低通滤波器（详细分析见第 B.3 节）。通过时间积分，它滤除梯度估计中固有的高频随机噪声与优化伪迹（例如小批量采样噪声、数值不稳定），从而提供更平滑的监督信号。这启发了我们的主要理论结果：匹配平均速度场足以实现分布对齐。

---

Intuitively, A VF describes the mean displacement vector
of a particle moving from position zs to zt over the time

<span id="S006-08"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-08

**Original:**

Here, p(s, t) denotes a distribution with full support on the
time domain, and the velocity fields are assumed to satisfy
Lipschitz continuity. The proof of Theorem 4.3 is provided
in Section C.2. As the interval [s, t] shrinks to a single
point (s→t ), the average velocity field converges to the
instantaneous. Thus, matching A VFs over intervals with full
temporal support implies matching instantaneous velocities
everywhere, which by Theorem 4.1 guaranteesP=Q.

**中文:**

这里 $p(s,t)$ 表示时间域上具有完全支撑的分布，并假设速度场满足 Lipschitz 连续性。定理 4.3 的证明见第 C.2 节。当区间 $[s,t]$ 收缩为单点（$s \to t$）时，平均速度场收敛到瞬时值。因此，在具有完全时间支撑的区间上匹配 AVF 意味着在所有位置匹配瞬时速度，而由定理 4.1 即保证 $P=Q$。

---

<span id="S006-09"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-09

**Original:**

Compared to instantaneous velocity matching, Mean Flow
Matching offers significant practical advantages. (1) The
temporal integration averages out noise in instantaneous
velocity estimates, reducing gradient variance (Section C.3).
(2) A VF captures cumulative trajectory behavior for global
consistency. We visualize MFD’s smooth velocity field in a
controlled 2D toy example (Section D.2).

**中文:**

与瞬时速度匹配相比，均值流匹配具有显著的实际优势。(1) 时间积分平均掉瞬时速度估计中的噪声，降低梯度方差（第 C.3 节）。(2) AVF 捕获累积轨迹行为以获得全局一致性。我们在一个受控的 2D 玩具示例（第 D.2 节）中可视化 MFD 的平滑速度场。

---

**Source:** p.6 S006-10

$$\nabla_\theta \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{MFD}} = \mathbb{E}\Big[(\nabla_\theta X_s)^\top \cdot \nabla_{X_s}\Delta(X_s)\cdot \Delta(X_s)\Big], \tag{18}$$

<span id="S006-22"></span>
**Source:** p.6 S006-22

**Original:**

The term ∇Xs∆(Xs) involves differentiating the output of
the ODE solverΦ with respect to the inputXs. This requires
backpropagation through the solver, which is computation-
ally expensive and potentially unstable. Following a simpli-
fication similar to Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) (Poole
et al., 2023), we treat the estimated average velocity fields
ˆU P and ˆU Q as fixed guidance targets at the current state
Xs. By omitting the Jacobian term (namely approximating
∇Xs∆(Xs)≈I effectively for the direction), the gradient
in Equation (18) is simplified to:

**中文:**

其中 $\nabla_{X_s}\Delta(X_s)$ 涉及对 ODE 求解器 $\Phi$ 的输出关于输入 $X_s$ 求导。这需要对求解器反向传播，计算昂贵且可能不稳定。参照 Score Distillation Sampling（SDS）（Poole et al., 2023）中的类似化简，我们把估计的平均速度场 $\hat{U}_P$ 与 $\hat{U}_Q$ 视为当前状态 $X_s$ 处的固定引导目标。通过省略雅可比项（即就方向而言有效近似 $\nabla_{X_s}\Delta(X_s) \approx I$），式 (18) 中的梯度简化为：

(∇θXs)⊤ ·∆(X s)
(19)

**中文:**

$$\nabla_\theta \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{MFD}} \approx \mathbb{E}\Big[(\nabla_\theta X_s)^\top \cdot \Delta(X_s)\Big], \tag{19}$$

$$\nabla_\theta \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{MFD}} \approx \mathbb{E}_{s,t,X_0}\Big[s\cdot(\nabla_\theta G_\theta(X_0))^\top\cdot\Big(\underbrace{\frac{\Phi(v_P, X_s, t, s) - X_s}{t-s}}_{\text{辅助均值流}} - \underbrace{\frac{\Phi(u_Q, X_s, t, s) - X_s}{t-s}}_{\text{教师均值流}}\Big)\Big]. \tag{20}$$

## PDF 第 7 页

<span id="S007-01"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-01

**Original:**

Algorithm 1Mean Flow Distillation (MFD)
Input:Pretrained teacher uQ, student Gθ, auxiliary flow
model vϕ, ODE solver Φ, iterations N, TTUR ratio K=
10
Output:Trained student modelG θ
forn= 1toNdo
// Update auxiliary flow model (Ksteps)
fork= 1toKdo
SampleX 0 ∼ N(0, I)
withno grad:
X1 =G θ(X0)// Student sample
ResampleX 0 ∼ N(0, I); Sampleτ∼ U[0,1]
Xτ = (1−τ)X 0 +τ X 1
Updateϕ:L F M =∥v ϕ(Xτ , τ)−(X 1 −X 0)∥2
2
end for
// Update student model (1 step)
SampleX 0 ∼ N(0, I);X 1 =G θ(X0)
ResampleX 0 ∼ N(0, I)
Samples, t∼p(s, t)with0≤s < t≤1
Xs = (1−s)X 0 +sX 1
withno grad:
ˆU Q = (Φ(uQ, Xs, t, s)−X s)/(t−s)
ˆU P = (Φ(vϕ, Xs, t, s)−X s)/(t−s)
Updateθ:L M F D = ( ˆU P − ˆU Q)·X s
end for
returnG θ

**中文:**

**算法 1：均值流蒸馏（MFD）**

**Input:** 预训练教师 $u_Q$、学生 $G_\theta$、辅助流模型 $v_\phi$、ODE 求解器 $\Phi$、迭代次数 $N$、TTUR 比例 $K = 10$

**Output:** 训练好的学生模型 $G_\theta$

**for** $n = 1$ **to** $N$ **do**
$\quad$ // 更新辅助流模型（$K$ 步）
$\quad$ **for** $k = 1$ **to** $K$ **do**
$\quad\quad$ 采样 $X_0 \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$
$\quad\quad$ **with no grad:**
$\quad\quad\quad$ $X_1 = G_\theta(X_0)$ // 学生样本
$\quad\quad$ 重新采样 $X_0 \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$；采样 $\tau \sim U[0,1]$
$\quad\quad$ $X_\tau = (1-\tau)X_0 + \tau X_1$
$\quad\quad$ 更新 $\phi$：$\mathcal{L}_{FM} = \|v_\phi(X_\tau, \tau) - (X_1 - X_0)\|_2^2$
$\quad$ **end for**
$\quad$ // 更新学生模型（1 步）
$\quad$ 采样 $X_0 \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$；$X_1 = G_\theta(X_0)$
$\quad$ 重新采样 $X_0 \sim \mathcal{N}(0, I)$
$\quad$ 采样 $s, t \sim p(s, t)$，使 $0 \le s < t \le 1$
$\quad$ $X_s = (1-s)X_0 + sX_1$
$\quad$ **with no grad:**
$\quad\quad$ $\hat{U}_Q = (\Phi(u_Q, X_s, t, s) - X_s)/(t-s)$
$\quad\quad$ $\hat{U}_P = (\Phi(v_\phi, X_s, t, s) - X_s)/(t-s)$
$\quad$ 更新 $\theta$：$\mathcal{L}_{MFD} = (\hat{U}_P - \hat{U}_Q)\cdot X_s$
**end for**
**return** $G_\theta$

> 伪代码标识符保留原文（如 with no grad、TTUR 比例 K=10）；原抽取的缩进与注释已按语义还原。

<span id="S007-02"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-02

**Original:**

Using Equation (20), the student model can be optimized
such that its single-step generation distribution closely
matches the teacher’s multi-step generation distribution. For
more discussion on the rationality and theoretical scope
of omitting the Jacobian term, please refer to Section B.6.

**中文:**

利用式 (20)，学生模型可以被优化到其单步生成分布与教师的多步生成分布紧密匹配。关于省略雅可比项的合理性与理论范围的更多讨论，请参见第 B.6 节。

---

<span id="S007-03"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-03

**Original:**

The complete training algorithm of Mean Flow Distillation
is summarized in Algorithm 1. Following the TTUR strat-
egy (Heusel et al., 2017) and existing distribution distillation
methods (Luo et al., 2023; Yin et al., 2024a), we set the al-
ternating update ratio between the auxiliary model and the
student model to 10:1, ensuring that the auxiliary model
can accurately track the evolving student distribution be-
fore each student update. Measured wall-clock and memory
costs are reported in Section 5.3.

**中文:**

均值流蒸馏的完整训练算法总结于算法 1。遵循 TTUR 策略（Heusel et al., 2017）与现有分布蒸馏方法（Luo et al., 2023; Yin et al., 2024a），我们把辅助模型与学生模型之间的交替更新比例设为 10:1，以确保在每次学生更新之前，辅助模型能准确跟踪演化中的学生分布。实测的墙钟时间与内存开销见第 5.3 节。

---

5. Experiments

two challenging scenarios, 4D occupancy forecasting for
autonomous driving (Section 5.1) and text-to-image gener-
ation (Section 5.2), to evaluate the effectiveness of MFD.
Detailed hyperparameters and training configurations are
provided in Section A.3.

**中文:**

本节在两类挑战性场景上开展一系列实验：自动驾驶中的 4D 占据预测（第 5.1 节）与文本到图像生成（第 5.2 节），以评估 MFD 的有效性。详细超参数与训练配置见第 A.3 节。

<span id="S007-07"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-07

**Original:**

5.1. 4D Occupancy Forecasting

**中文:**

### 5.1 4D 占据预测

---

<span id="S007-08"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-08

**Original:**

4D occupancy forecasting is a critical perception task in
autonomous driving that predicts future occupancy states
of the surrounding environment in a spatiotemporal vol-
ume (Liu et al., 2025). This task requires modeling complex
scene dynamics, making it an ideal testbed for evaluating
generative model distillation under real-world constraints.

**中文:**

4D 占据预测是自动驾驶中的一项关键感知任务，它在时空体中预测周围环境的未来占据状态（Liu et al., 2025）。该任务需要建模复杂的场景动力学，使其成为在真实约束下评估生成模型蒸馏的理想试验平台。

---

<span id="S007-09"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-09

**Original:**

We conduct experiments on OccFM (Liu et al., 2025) for
LiDAR-based 4D occupancy prediction, initializing the
teacher, auxiliary, and student models from the same pre-
trained checkpoint. Experiments are conducted on the
nuScenes dataset (Caesar et al., 2020), with performance
measured by IoU and mean IoU (mIoU) (Song et al.,
2017). We compare against advanced baselines, includ-
ing Consistency Models (Song et al., 2023), Align Your
Flow (AYF) (Sabour et al., 2025), Diff-Instruct (Luo et al.,
2023) (VSD-based), and SOTA distillation methods such as
DMD2 (Yin et al., 2024a) and SenseFlow (Ge et al., 2026).

**中文:**

我们在 OccFM（Liu et al., 2025）上开展基于 LiDAR 的 4D 占据预测实验，并从同一预训练检查点初始化教师、辅助与学生模型。实验在 nuScenes 数据集（Caesar et al., 2020）上进行，用 IoU 与平均 IoU（mIoU）（Song et al., 2017）衡量性能。我们与先进基线比较，包括一致性模型（Song et al., 2023）、Align Your Flow（AYF）（Sabour et al., 2025）、Diff-Instruct（Luo et al., 2023）（基于 VSD），以及 DMD2（Yin et al., 2024a）与 SenseFlow（Ge et al., 2026）等 SOTA 蒸馏方法。

---

<span id="S007-10"></span>
**Source:** p.7 S007-10

**Original:**

Table 1 presents a comprehensive comparison of MFD
against the teacher model and baseline distillation methods.
Compared with the teacher model with 10-steps sampling,
the proposed MFD achieves remarkable distillation quality,
losing only1.2%of the teacher’s IoU (37.07% vs. 37.52%)
and4.4%of its mIoU (27.25% vs. 28.49%) while increas-
ing the sampling speedtenfold( reducing NFE from 10 to 1)
and increasing the FPS from 12.72 to 25.21. Moreover, com-
pared to teacher models with fewer sampling steps, MFD
achieves higher generation quality.

**中文:**

表 1 给出 MFD 与教师模型及基线蒸馏方法的全面比较。与进行 10 步采样的教师模型相比，所提 MFD 取得显著的蒸馏质量：仅损失教师 IoU 的 1.2%（37.07% vs. 37.52%）与 mIoU 的 4.4%（27.25% vs. 28.49%），同时把采样速度提升十倍（NFE 从 10 降到 1），FPS 从 12.72 提升到 25.21。此外，与采样步数更少的教师模型相比，MFD 取得更高的生成质量。

---

**Source:** p.7 S007-11

**Original:**

Compared with other distillation methods, MFD achieves
the optimal performance across all metrics. These perfor-
mance gaps highlight fundamental limitations of existing
methods. VSD suffers from high-variance gradients due to
its reliance on single-timestep velocity estimates, which are
noisy in high-dimensional latent spaces and fail to capture
the global trajectory structure of flow matching. DMD2 and
SenseFlow introduce adversarial discriminators, leading to
training instability and strong hyperparameter sensitivity,
especially when adapting to flow matching models with
7

**中文:**

与其他蒸馏方法相比，MFD 在所有指标上都取得最优性能。这些性能差距凸显了现有方法的根本局限。VSD 因依赖单时间步速度估计而受高方差梯度困扰，这种估计在高维隐空间中噪声很大，且无法捕获 Flow Matching 的全局轨迹结构。DMD2 与 SenseFlow 引入对抗判别器，导致训练不稳定与强超参数敏感性，尤其是在适配具有

<span id="S008-02"></span>
**Source:** p.8 S008-02

**Original:**

different loss landscapes. Moreover, both still depend on in-
stantaneous score matching, which struggles to model long-
horizon flow dynamics. Consistency-based methods, in-
cluding CD and AYF, impose sample-level self-consistency
constraints that prove overly restrictive for complex multi-
modal data; MFD outperforms AYF by 4.69 IoU and 5.56
mIoU. In contrast, MFD matches time-integrated mean
flows at the distribution level, yielding low-variance gra-
dients that preserve generation quality while ensuring stable
training. All measured single-step methods achieve similar
inference speeds (≈25 FPS), indicating that although MFD
introduces an auxiliary flow model vϕ during training, it
incurs negligible inference overhead. Qualitative visualiza-
tions are provided in Figure 5 in the appendix.

**中文:**

不同损失地貌的 Flow Matching 模型时（承接第 7 页末句）。此外，两者仍依赖瞬时分数匹配，这难以建模长时段流的动力学。基于一致性的方法（包括 CD 与 AYF）施加样本级自一致性约束，对复杂的多模态数据而言过于严格；MFD 比 AYF 高出 4.69 IoU 与 5.56 mIoU。相反，MFD 在分布层面匹配时间积分的均值流，产生低方差梯度，在保证稳定训练的同时保持生成质量。所有实测的单步方法达到相近的推理速度（约 25 FPS），说明尽管 MFD 在训练时引入辅助流模型 $v_\phi$，其推理开销可以忽略。定性可视化见附录图 5。

> 承接 S007-11 的跨页断句。

---

<span id="S008-03"></span>
**Source:** p.8 S008-03

**Original:**

To investigate the sensitivity of MFD to the numerical ac-
curacy of mean flow computation, we vary the ODE solver
step size in Φ(v, Xs, t, s). Smaller steps reduce discretiza-
tion error but increase training cost. Results in Table 2
reveal an interesting non-monotonic trend. Very small steps
(0.001) hurt performance due to accumulated numerical er-
rors. Moderate steps (0.005–0.02) perform best, with 0.02
achieving the highest mIoU, indicating that a coarser inte-
gration provides more robust supervision by averaging noisy
velocities. Large steps (0.1–0.5) degrade performance, as
too few integration steps fail to capture trajectory curvature.
As ∆τ approaches (t−s) , MFD collapses to a single-step,
VSD-like form, losing the variance-reduction benefit of tem-
poral integration. In practice we adopt0.02.

**中文:**

为考察 MFD 对均值流计算数值精度的敏感性，我们改变 $\Phi(v, X_s, t, s)$ 中的 ODE 求解器步长。更小的步长减小离散化误差但增加训练成本。表 2 的结果揭示出一个有趣的非单调趋势。极小的步长（0.001）因累积的数值误差而损害性能。中等步长（0.005–0.02）表现最好，其中 0.02 取得最高 mIoU，说明更粗的积分通过对噪声速度取平均而提供了更鲁棒的监督。大步行（0.1–0.5）会降低性能，因为积分步太少无法捕获轨迹曲率。当 $\Delta\tau$ 趋近 $(t-s)$ 时，MFD 坍缩为单步、类 VSD 的形式，失去时间积分带来的方差降低收益。实践中我们取 0.02。

---

5.2. Text-to-Image Generation

**中文:**

### 5.2 文本到图像生成

---

<span id="S008-05"></span>
**Source:** p.8 S008-05

**Original:**

Text-to-image generation is a fundamental task in generative
modeling that synthesizes images from natural language
descriptions. Its high-dimensional output space makes it an
ideal benchmark for evaluating distillation methods.

**中文:**

文本到图像生成是生成建模中的一项基础任务，它从自然语言描述合成图像。其高维输出空间使其成为评估蒸馏方法的理想基准。

---

<span id="S008-06"></span>
**Source:** p.8 S008-06

**Original:**

We conduct experiments on SANA 1.6B (Xie et al., 2025),
a state-of-the-art flow matching model for text-to-image
generation, where the teacher model, auxiliary flow model,
and student generator are all initialized from the pretrained
SANA checkpoint. We train and evaluate on the LAION-
aesthetic-6.5+ (Schuhmann et al., 2022) dataset using five
complementary metrics: Aesthetic Score (Schuhmann et al.,
2022) for visual appeal, PickScore (Kirstain et al., 2023)
for human preference prediction, HPSv2 (Wu et al., 2023)

**中文:**

我们在 SANA 1.6B（Xie et al., 2025）上开展实验，这是一个用于文本到图像生成的最先进 Flow Matching 模型，其中教师模型、辅助流模型与学生生成器都由预训练的 SANA 检查点初始化。我们在 LAION-aesthetic-6.5+（Schuhmann et al., 2022）数据集上训练与评估，使用五项互补指标：视觉吸引力用 Aesthetic Score（Schuhmann et al., 2022），人类偏好预测用 PickScore（Kirstain et al., 2023），美学对齐用 HPSv2（Wu et al., 2023）

for aesthetic alignment, ImageReward (Xu et al., 2023)
for overall quality and text-image alignment, and CLIP

ditionally report FID with DINOv2 features (Oquab et al.,

sure distributional proximity. We compare MFD against

---

<span id="S008-09"></span>
**Source:** p.8 S008-09

**Original:**

Table 3 presents a comprehensive comparison on text-
to-image generation. MFD achieves the best Aesthetic
Score, ImageReward, CLIP Score, and FID(DINOv2), while
matching the strongest baselines on PickScore and HPSv2
within a small margin. Compared with AYF, MFD improves
all reported metrics and reduces FID from 43.68 to 33.28,
indicating that distribution-level mean-flow alignment pro-
vides stronger few-step distillation than sample-level con-
sistency constraints. DMD2 and SenseFlow achieve slightly
higher PickScore or HPSv2, but they incorporate additional
components such as perceptual losses or adversarial training
(GAN) (Goodfellow et al., 2020). In contrast, MFD focuses
purely on distribution matching through mean flow align-
ment as a foundational distillation approach, and can be com-
bined with perceptual losses (Johnson et al., 2016) or adver-
sarial objectives in future work. Coverage-oriented CIFAR-
10 precision/recall results are provided in Section D.1.

**中文:**

表 3 给出文本到图像生成的全面比较。MFD 取得最佳的 Aesthetic Score、ImageReward、CLIP Score 与 FID(DINOv2)，同时在 PickScore 与 HPSv2 上以很小差距与最强基线持平。与 AYF 相比，MFD 改善了所有报告指标并把 FID 从 43.68 降到 33.28，说明分布级的均值流对齐比样本级一致性约束提供了更强的少步蒸馏。DMD2 与 SenseFlow 取得略高的 PickScore 或 HPSv2，但它们引入了额外成分，如感知损失或对抗训练（GAN）（Goodfellow et al., 2020）。相反，MFD 作为一项基础蒸馏方法，纯粹聚焦于通过均值流对齐实现分布匹配，未来可与感知损失（Johnson et al., 2016）或对抗目标结合。面向覆盖度的 CIFAR-10 精确率/召回率结果见第 D.1 节。

<span id="S009-03"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-03

**Original:**

Similarly, we investigate the sensitivity of MFD to the nu-
merical accuracy of mean flow computation in the text-to-
image generation setting. Table 7 reports the performance of
MFD under different ODE solver step sizes, which control
the discretization granularity used in mean flow estimation.
The ablation study on ODE solver step size reveals consis-
tent trends with the 4D occupancy forecasting experiments.
Excessively small step sizes result in degraded performance,
likely due to the accumulation of numerical errors over a
large number of integration steps. Due to the length limita-
tion, the detailed analysis is shown in Section D.4. Empiri-
cally, we recommend the ODE solver step size be set to0.02
or 0.03. Additionally, we present qualitative comparisons
of generated images in Figure 8, demonstrating the visual
quality of MFD-distilled models with 4-step inference.

**中文:**

类似地，我们考察 MFD 在文本到图像生成设置中对均值流计算数值精度的敏感性。表 7 报告了不同 ODE 求解器步长下 MFD 的表现，这些步长控制均值流估计中使用的离散化粒度。关于 ODE 求解器步长的消融实验揭示出与 4D 占据预测实验一致的趋势。过小的步长会导致性能下降，很可能是由于在大量积分步上累积的数值误差。受篇幅限制，详细分析见第 D.4 节。在经验上，我们建议把 ODE 求解器步长设为 0.02 或 0.03。此外，我们在图 8 中给出生成图像的定性比较，展示 MFD 蒸馏模型在 4 步推理下的视觉质量。

5.3. Training Cost and Practical Overhead

<span id="S009-05"></span>

The auxiliary flow model vϕ is discarded at inference, so
MFD incurs no extra inference cost (cf. Table 1). Imple-
mented with LoRA (rank 64) on SANA 1.6B, it adds only
∼34M trainable parameters (<2.2% of the backbone). Ta-
ble 4 compares wall-clock cost and peak GPU memory:
MFD (∼25 GPU hours, ∼20 GB on one RTX PRO 6000)
is comparable to VSD while using substantially less mem-
ory than DMD2, which additionally trains a discriminator.

**中文:**

辅助流模型 $v_\phi$ 在推理时被丢弃，因此 MFD 不产生额外的推理开销（对比表 1）。在 SANA 1.6B 上以 LoRA（秩 64）实现时，它仅增加约 34M 可训练参数（< 2.2% 的主干）。表 4 比较了墙钟成本与峰值 GPU 内存：MFD（约 25 GPU 小时，在单张 RTX PRO 6000 上约 20 GB）与 VSD 相当，而内存占用显著低于还要额外训练判别器的 DMD2。

<span id="S009-09"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-09

**Original:**

6. Conclusion

**中文:**

## 6 结论

---

<span id="S009-10"></span>
**Source:** p.9 S009-10

**Original:**

In this paper, we presented Mean Flow Distillation (MFD),
a robust framework that addresses the high gradient vari-
ance inherent in score-based distillation by aligning time-
integrated average velocity fields. Theoretically grounded
in our Mean Flow Matching Theorem, MFD functions as a
temporal low-pass filter, suppressing optimization noise to
ensure global trajectory consistency. Extensive experiments
on 4D occupancy forecasting and text-to-image generation
demonstrate that MFD consistently outperforms state-of-
the-art baselines, achieving high-fidelity single-step gen-
eration with superior training stability and distributional
fidelity. While our current approach relies on an auxiliary
flow model during training, it adds no inference overhead;
future work will further reduce its training cost and broaden
the framework to additional flow matching domains.

**中文:**

本文提出均值流蒸馏（MFD），一个鲁棒的框架：通过对齐时间积分的平均速度场，解决基于分数的蒸馏中固有的高梯度方差问题。MFD 以我们的均值流匹配定理为理论基础，充当时间低通滤波器，抑制优化噪声以保证全局轨迹一致性。在 4D 占据预测与文本到图像生成上的大量实验表明，MFD 一致优于最先进的基线，在具有更优训练稳定性与分布保真度的同时实现高保真单步生成。虽然我们当前的方法在训练时依赖辅助流模型，但它不增加推理开销；未来工作将进一步降低其训练成本，并把该框架扩展到更多 Flow Matching 领域。

## PDF 第 10 页

<span id="S010-01"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-01

**Original:**

Impact Statement

**中文:**

## 影响声明

---

<span id="S010-02"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-02

**Original:**

This paper presents work whose goal is to advance the field
of Machine Learning, specifically in accelerating generative
models through flow matching distillation. The primary
societal impact of our work lies in democratizing access to
high-quality generative AI by significantly reducing com-
putational costs. By enabling single-step generation with
minimal quality loss, MFD can make powerful text-to-image
and 4D perception models more accessible to researchers
and practitioners with limited computational resources, po-
tentially fostering broader innovation in creative industries
and autonomous systems.

**中文:**

本文呈现的工作旨在推进机器学习领域，特别是通过 Flow Matching 蒸馏加速生成模型。我们工作的主要社会影响在于通过显著降低计算成本来普及高质量生成式 AI 的可及性。通过以极小的质量损失实现单步生成，MFD 能使强大的文本到图像与 4D 感知模型更易被计算资源有限的研究者与从业者使用，从而有望促进创意产业与自动驾驶系统中的更广泛创新。

---

<span id="S010-03"></span>
**Source:** p.10 S010-03

**Original:**

However, we acknowledge potential dual-use concerns. Ac-
celerated generative models could facilitate the rapid cre-
ation of synthetic media, which may be misused for generat-
ing misleading or harmful content. We encourage practition-
ers deploying MFD-distilled models to implement appro-
priate safeguards, including content filtering, watermarking,
and provenance tracking mechanisms. In the context of au-
tonomous driving applications, while our method improves
efficiency, thorough safety validation and testing remain
essential before real-world deployment. Overall, we be-
lieve the benefits of improved accessibility and efficiency
outweigh the risks when accompanied by responsible de-
ployment practices.

**中文:**

然而，我们注意到潜在的两用风险。加速的生成模型可能助长合成媒体的快速创作，而这些内容可能被滥用于生成误导性或有害信息。我们鼓励部署 MFD 蒸馏模型的从业者实施适当的防护措施，包括内容过滤、水印与来源追踪机制。在自动驾驶应用场景中，尽管我们的方法提升了效率，但在真实部署之前仍必须进行充分的安全验证与测试。总体而言，我们认为在配合负责任的部署实践时，可及性与效率提升带来的收益超过其风险。

---

## PDF 第 13 页

<span id="S013-01"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-01

A. Experiment protocol

**中文:**

## A 实验方案

---

<span id="S013-02"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-02

**Original:**

A.1. Dataset setup

**中文:**

### A.1 数据集设置

---

<span id="S013-03"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-03

**Original:**

nuScenes (Caesar et al., 2020) is a large-scale multimodal perception dataset for autonomous driving, released by Motional
(formerly nuTonomy), and represents the first public dataset to systematically provide a full-suite sensor configuration
for autonomous vehicles. Collected in real-world urban driving scenarios, nuScenes comprises synchronized data from
six surround-view cameras, one 32-beam LiDAR, five millimeter-wave radars, along with high-precision GPS and IMU
measurements, enabling multi-view and multimodal modeling in complex traffic environments. Compared to earlier datasets
such as KITTI, nuScenes offers substantially broader sensor coverage as well as significant improvements in data scale and
annotation density. It contains over seven times more object-level annotations than KITTI and provides long-term temporal
sequences with fine-grained 3D object labels, attributes, and motion states. Owing to its rich multimodal temporal data and
high-quality 3D annotations, nuScenes has become a widely adopted benchmark for tasks such as 4D occupancy forecasting,
temporal scene understanding, and world modeling.

**中文:**

nuScenes（Caesar et al., 2020）是一个面向自动驾驶的大规模多模态感知数据集，由 Motional（原 nuTonomy）发布，是首个系统性地为自动驾驶车辆提供全套传感器配置的公开数据集。nuScenes 采集自真实城市驾驶场景，包含来自六个环视相机、一台 32 线 LiDAR、五台毫米波雷达的同步数据，以及高精度 GPS 与 IMU 测量，可在复杂交通环境中支持多视角与多模态建模。与 KITTI 等更早的数据集相比，nuScenes 提供显著更广的传感器覆盖，并在数据规模与标注密度上有明显提升。它包含的对象级标注数量是 KITTI 的七倍以上，并提供带有细粒度 3D 物体标签、属性与运动状态的长期时序序列。凭借丰富的多模态时序数据与高质量 3D 标注，nuScenes 已成为 4D 占据预测、时序场景理解与世界建模等任务中被广泛采用的基准。

---

<span id="S013-04"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-04

**Original:**

LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ (Schuhmann et al., 2022) is a high-quality subset of the LAION-5B dataset, specifically curated
for training and evaluating text-to-image generation models. LAION (Large-scale Artificial Intelligence Open Network)
is one of the largest publicly available image-text pair datasets, collected from the public web through Common Crawl.
The aesthetic-6.5+ subset applies an aesthetic quality filter based on the LAION aesthetic predictor, retaining only images
with predicted aesthetic scores of 6.5 or higher (on a scale from 1 to 10). This filtering process ensures that the dataset
contains visually appealing, high-quality images with good composition, color harmony, and overall aesthetic appeal,
making it particularly suitable for training generative models that aim to produce visually pleasing outputs. Each sample in
LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ consists of an image paired with its corresponding alt-text caption scraped from the web, providing
natural language descriptions that range from short phrases to detailed sentences. The dataset covers an extremely diverse
range of visual concepts, including everyday objects, natural scenes, portraits, abstract art, architectural structures, and
various artistic styles. This diversity in both visual content and textual descriptions enables text-to-image models to learn
robust cross-modal alignment and generate high-fidelity images across a wide spectrum of prompts. Due to its large scale,
high aesthetic quality, and rich semantic diversity, LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ has become a de facto standard benchmark for
training and fine-tuning state-of-the-art text-to-image generation models, including Stable Diffusion, DALL-E variants, and
flow-based generative models.

**中文:**

LAION-aesthetic-6.5+（Schuhmann et al., 2022）是 LAION-5B 数据集的一个高质量子集，专门为训练与评估文本到图像生成模型而整理。LAION（Large-scale Artificial Intelligence Open Network）是最大的公开图文对数据集之一，通过 Common Crawl 从公开网络收集。aesthetic-6.5+ 子集应用基于 LAION 美学预测器的美学质量过滤，只保留预测美学分数不低于 6.5（1 到 10 分制）的图像。该过滤过程确保数据集包含视觉上吸引人、构图良好、色彩和谐且整体美学吸引力高的高质量图像，使其特别适合训练旨在产出视觉愉悦输出的生成模型。LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ 中的每个样本由一张图像及其从网络抓取的对应 alt-text 描述组成，提供从短语到详细句子的自然语言描述。该数据集覆盖极其多样的视觉概念，包括日常物体、自然场景、人像、抽象艺术、建筑结构与多种艺术风格。这种在视觉内容与文本描述上的多样性，使文本到图像模型能够学到鲁棒的跨模态对齐，并在广泛提示词范围内生成高保真图像。凭借其大规模、高美学质量与丰富的语义多样性，LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ 已成为训练与微调最先进文本到图像生成模型（包括 Stable Diffusion、DALL-E 变体以及基于流的生成模型）的事实标准基准。

---

<span id="S013-05"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-05

**Original:**

A.2. Evaluation metrics

**中文:**

### A.2 评价指标

---

<span id="S013-06"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-06

**Original:**

Intersection over Union (IoU) (Song et al., 2017) is a classical evaluation metric that measures the degree of overlap between
model predictions and ground-truth annotations, and is widely used in tasks such as object detection, semantic segmentation,
and occupancy prediction. It is defined as the ratio between the intersection and the union of the predicted region and the
ground-truth region, taking values in the range [0,1] , where higher values indicate better agreement with the ground truth.
In 3D or 4D scene understanding tasks, IoU can be naturally extended to voxel-level or spatiotemporal volume-level IoU,
enabling the evaluation of a model’s ability to capture spatial structures and their temporal evolution. As such, IoU serves as
a fundamental metric for assessing prediction quality at the level of individual classes or samples.

**中文:**

交并比（Intersection over Union, IoU）（Song et al., 2017）是一种经典评价指标，度量模型预测与真值标注之间的重叠程度，广泛用于目标检测、语义分割与占据预测等任务。它定义为预测区域与真值区域的交集与并集之比，取值在 $[0,1]$ 范围内，数值越高表示与真值越一致。在 3D 或 4D 场景理解任务中，IoU 可以自然扩展到体素级或时空体级 IoU，从而能够评估模型捕获空间结构及其时序演化的能力。因此，IoU 是在单个类别或样本层面评估预测质量的基础指标。

---

<span id="S013-07"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-07

**Original:**

Mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) aggregates IoU scores across multiple semantic classes or time steps to provide
a holistic measure of overall model performance. Specifically, mIoU is typically computed by first evaluating IoU for
each semantic category (or occupancy state) independently and then taking the arithmetic mean over all categories. This
averaging strategy prevents the evaluation from being dominated by classes with a large number of samples. Compared to
a single IoU score, mIoU more effectively reflects a model’s class-wise balance and robustness, and is therefore widely
adopted as a core evaluation metric in semantic segmentation, occupancy grid prediction, and 4D occupancy forecasting
tasks.

**中文:**

平均交并比（Mean Intersection over Union, mIoU）在多个语义类别或时间步上聚合 IoU 分数，以给出整体模型性能的全面度量。具体地，mIoU 通常先对每个语义类别（或占据状态）独立计算 IoU，再对所有类别取算术平均。这种平均策略避免评估被样本数量很多的类别所主导。与单一 IoU 分数相比，mIoU 更有效地反映模型的类别间平衡性与鲁棒性，因此在语义分割、占据栅格预测与 4D 占据预测任务中被广泛采用为核心评价指标。

---

<span id="S013-08"></span>
**Source:** p.13 S013-08

**Original:**

Aesthetic Score (Schuhmann et al., 2022) is an automated image quality assessment metric developed as part of the LAION
project to predict the aesthetic appeal of generated images. It is trained to predict human aesthetic preferences on a scale
from 1 to 10, with higher scores indicating more visually pleasing images in terms of composition, color harmony, lighting,
and overall artistic quality. The predictor is trained on a large dataset of images with human-annotated aesthetic ratings,
enabling it to serve as a proxy for human aesthetic judgment. This metric is particularly useful for evaluating text-to-image
generation models, as it captures subjective visual quality aspects that go beyond simple semantic alignment or technical
correctness.

**中文:**

Aesthetic Score（Schuhmann et al., 2022）是作为 LAION 项目一部分开发的自动图像质量评估指标，用于预测生成图像的美学吸引力。它被训练来预测人的美学偏好，分值为 1 到 10，分数越高表示图像在构图、色彩和谐、光照与整体艺术质量上更具视觉吸引力。该预测器在带有美学人工评分的大规模图像数据集上训练，因此可以作为人类美学判断的代理。该指标对评估文本到图像生成模型特别有用，因为它捕获了超越简单语义对齐或技术正确性的主观视觉质量方面。

---

[第 13 页](#page-13)

<span id="page-14"></span>
## PDF 第 14 页

<span id="S014-01"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-01

**Original:**

PickScore (Kirstain et al., 2023) is a human preference prediction metric specifically designed for text-to-image generation
evaluation. Unlike traditional metrics that focus on individual image attributes, PickScore is trained to predict which
image humans would prefer when presented with pairs of generated images for the same text prompt. The metric is
based on a vision-language model fine-tuned on a large-scale dataset of human preference comparisons collected through
crowdsourcing. PickScore provides a scalar value that correlates with human preference rankings, making it a reliable
indicator of generation quality from a human-centered perspective. This metric is particularly valuable for assessing the
overall appeal and naturalness of generated images beyond pixel-level or feature-level similarity measures.

**中文:**

PickScore（Kirstain et al., 2023）是专为文本到图像生成评估设计的人类偏好预测指标。与关注单个图像属性的传统指标不同，PickScore 被训练来预测：对同一文本提示给出成对生成图像时，人类会更偏好哪一张。该指标基于一个视觉-语言模型，该模型在通过众包收集的大规模人类偏好比较数据集上微调。PickScore 给出与人类偏好排序相关的标量值，使其成为从以人为中心视角衡量生成质量的可靠指标。该指标对于评估生成图像超出像素级或特征级相似度的整体吸引力与自然度尤其有价值。

---

<span id="S014-02"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-02

**Original:**

HPSv2 (Human Preference Score v2) (Wu et al., 2023) is an advanced human preference prediction metric that represents
the second generation of human preference scoring systems for text-to-image generation. Building upon the original HPS
framework, HPSv2 incorporates improved training strategies and a more diverse preference dataset to better capture the
nuanced aspects of human aesthetic judgment. The metric evaluates multiple dimensions of image quality including aesthetic
appeal, prompt alignment, photorealism, and artistic coherence. HPSv2 demonstrates strong correlation with human
evaluations across diverse generation scenarios and has become widely adopted as a standard benchmark for comparing
text-to-image generation models.

**中文:**

HPSv2（Human Preference Score v2）（Wu et al., 2023）是一种先进的人类偏好预测指标，代表文本到图像生成人类偏好评分体系的第二代。在原始 HPS 框架基础上，HPSv2 引入改进的训练策略与更多样的偏好数据集，以更好捕获人类美学判断的细微方面。该指标从多个维度评估图像质量，包括美学吸引力、提示词对齐、照片真实感与艺术连贯性。HPSv2 在多种生成场景下与人工评价表现出强相关性，已成为比较文本到图像生成模型时被广泛采用的标准基准。

---

<span id="S014-03"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-03

**Original:**

ImageReward (Xu et al., 2023) is a comprehensive reward function designed to holistically assess the quality of text-to-image
generation by jointly considering multiple aspects including visual fidelity, semantic alignment with text prompts, aesthetic
quality, and overall human preference. Unlike single-dimensional metrics, ImageReward is trained using reinforcement
learning from human feedback (RLHF) on a large-scale dataset of human annotations that capture diverse quality aspects.
The metric provides a unified score that balances technical correctness (e.g., object presence, spatial relationships) with
subjective quality judgments (e.g., artistic appeal, naturalness). ImageReward has been shown to correlate strongly with
comprehensive human evaluations and is particularly effective for optimizing text-to-image models through reward-based
fine-tuning strategies.

**中文:**

ImageReward（Xu et al., 2023）是一个综合奖励函数，通过联合考虑视觉保真度、与文本提示的语义对齐、美学质量与整体人类偏好等多个方面，整体评估文本到图像生成的质量。与单维指标不同，ImageReward 使用基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）在捕获多样质量方面的大规模人工标注数据集上训练。该指标给出统一分数，在技术正确性（例如物体是否存在、空间关系）与主观质量判断（例如艺术吸引力、自然度）之间取得平衡。ImageReward 已被证明与综合人工评价强相关，并特别适用于通过基于奖励的微调策略优化文本到图像模型。

---

<span id="S014-04"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-04

**Original:**

CLIP Score (Hessel et al., 2021) measures the semantic alignment between generated images and their corresponding text
prompts using the CLIP (Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training) model. CLIP is trained on hundreds of millions of
image-text pairs from the internet to learn joint embeddings where semantically related images and texts are mapped to
nearby points in a shared representation space. The CLIP Score is computed as the cosine similarity between the CLIP
embeddings of a generated image and its text prompt, providing a quantitative measure of how well the image content
matches the textual description. Higher CLIP Scores indicate stronger semantic correspondence, capturing whether the
generated image contains the correct objects, attributes, actions, and relationships specified in the prompt. While CLIP
Score primarily evaluates semantic fidelity rather than aesthetic quality or photorealism, it serves as a fundamental metric
for assessing the text-image alignment capability of generation models and has become a standard evaluation tool in the
text-to-image generation literature.

**中文:**

CLIP Score（Hessel et al., 2021）使用 CLIP（Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training）模型度量生成图像与其对应文本提示之间的语义对齐。CLIP 在数亿互联网图文对上训练，学习联合嵌入，使语义相关的图像与文本被映射到共享表示空间中相近的点。CLIP Score 计算生成图像的 CLIP 嵌入与其文本提示的 CLIP 嵌入之间的余弦相似度，从而定量衡量图像内容与文本描述的匹配程度。CLIP Score 越高表示语义对应越强，可反映生成图像是否包含提示词所指定的正确物体、属性、动作与关系。尽管 CLIP Score 主要评估语义保真度而非美学质量或照片真实感，它仍是评估生成模型文图对齐能力的基础指标，并已成为文本到图像生成文献中的标准评估工具。

---

<span id="S014-05"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-05

**Original:**

Fr´echet Inception Distance (FID) measures the distance between feature distributions of generated and reference images by
fitting Gaussian distributions to their extracted features. In our text-to-image experiments, we use DINOv2 features (Oquab
et al., 2024) and compute FID against teacher-generated reference samples, which directly evaluates how closely the
student preserves the teacher distribution. For CIFAR-10 coverage analysis, we additionally report precision and re-
call (Kynk¨a¨anniemi et al., 2019): precision measures sample fidelity by estimating how much of the generated distribution
lies on the data manifold, while recall measures coverage by estimating how much of the reference distribution is recovered
by the generator.

**中文:**

Fréchet Inception Distance（FID）通过对生成图像与参考图像提取的特征拟合高斯分布，度量二者特征分布之间的距离。在本文的文本到图像实验中，我们使用 DINOv2 特征（Oquab et al., 2024），并针对教师生成的参考样本计算 FID，这直接评估学生模型对教师分布的保持程度。对于 CIFAR-10 覆盖度分析，我们另外报告精确率与召回率（Kynkäänniemi et al., 2019）：精确率通过估计生成分布有多大比例落在数据流形上来衡量样本保真度，而召回率通过估计生成器恢复了参考分布的多少来衡量覆盖度。

---

<span id="S014-06"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-06

**Original:**

A.3. Implementation details

**中文:**

### A.3 实现细节

---

<span id="S014-07"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-07

**Original:**

A.3.1. 4D OCCUPANCYFORECASTING

**中文:**

#### A.3.1 4D 占据预测

---

<span id="S014-08"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-08

**Original:**

During the training, the variational autoencoder (V AE) used for latent space encoding is kept frozen with pretrained weights
throughout training. Except for the auxiliary model and student model, all other network parameters remain frozen during
distillation. The student model is configured as a single-step generator Gθ :R d →R d, directly mapping noise to the final
occupancy prediction without iterative refinement.

**中文:**

训练期间，用于隐空间编码的变分自编码器（VAE）保持冻结并沿用预训练权重。除辅助模型与学生模型外，其他所有网络参数在蒸馏过程中保持冻结。学生模型配置为单步生成器 $G_\theta: \mathbb{R}^d \to \mathbb{R}^d$，直接把噪声映射为最终的占据预测，无需迭代精化。

---

<span id="S014-09"></span>
**Source:** p.14 S014-09

**Original:**

We employ the Adam optimizer with a learning rate of 1×10 −5 for both the auxiliary flow model vϕ and the student model
Gθ, incorporating a linear warmup schedule over the first 100 iterations to stabilize early-stage training. Gradient clipping
with a maximum norm of 1.0 is applied. Training is conducted on a single node equipped with 8 NVIDIA RTX 3090 GPUs
using distributed data parallel (DDP). We set the batch size to 1 per GPU (effective batch size of 8), with mixed-precision
14

**中文:**

我们对辅助流模型 $v_\phi$ 与学生模型 $G_\theta$ 均采用 Adam 优化器，学习率为 $1\times10^{-5}$，并在前 100 次迭代使用线性预热调度以稳定早期训练。施加最大范数为 1.0 的梯度裁剪。训练在配备 8 块 NVIDIA RTX 3090 GPU 的单节点上使用分布式数据并行（DDP）进行。我们把每 GPU 的批大小设为 1（有效批大小 8），并启用混合精度

> 第 14 页末被截断，续接第 15 页 S015-01。

---

[第 14 页](#page-14)

<span id="page-15"></span>
## PDF 第 15 页

<span id="S015-01"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-01

**Original:**

training (FP16) enabled to accelerate computation and reduce memory consumption using a gradient rescaling factor of 10.0.
Following recent findings in diffusion model training, we adopt a logit-normal biased sampling strategy (Esser et al., 2024)
for timesteps t and s, which allocates more sampling density to intermediate timesteps where the velocity field exhibits
higher variance. When computing the mean flow ˆU(X s, s, t), we employ an adaptive ODE solver with a maximum step
size of 0.02 to ensure accurate numerical integration along the velocity field—our ablation study validates this choice as an
optimal trade-off between integration accuracy and computational efficiency. During distillation, the teacher modeluQ is
evaluated with classifier-free guidance (CFG) enabled at a guidance scale of w= 2.0 to enhance conditional generation
quality and provide stronger supervision signals, while the auxiliary flow model vϕ is trained without CFG to directly model
the student distribution P . We train for a total of 30 epochs on the nuScenes training set (approximately 28,000 annotated
keyframes) and use the final checkpoint for evaluation, as we observe minimal overfitting due to the regularization provided
by the pretrained initialization and frozen V AE encoder.

**中文:**

训练（FP16）以加速计算并降低内存消耗，梯度缩放因子为 10.0（承接上页）。参照扩散模型训练中的近期发现，我们对时间步 $t$ 与 $s$ 采用 logit-normal 偏置采样策略（Esser et al., 2024），它把更多采样密度分配给速度场方差更高的中间时间步。在计算均值流 $\hat{U}(X_s, s, t)$ 时，我们采用最大步长为 0.02 的自适应 ODE 求解器，以确保沿速度场的数值积分精度——我们的消融研究验证了该选择是积分精度与计算效率之间的最优折中。在蒸馏过程中，教师模型 $u_Q$ 在启用无分类器引导（CFG）、引导尺度 $w = 2.0$ 下评估，以提升条件生成质量并提供更强的监督信号，而辅助流模型 $v_\phi$ 在没有 CFG 的情况下训练，以直接建模学生分布 $P$。我们在 nuScenes 训练集（约 28,000 个带标注关键帧）上共训练 30 个 epoch，并使用最终检查点进行评估，因为我们观察到由于预训练初始化与冻结 VAE 编码器提供的正则化，过拟合很小。

> 承接 S014-09 的跨页断句。

---

<span id="S015-02"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-02

**Original:**

A.3.2. TEXT-TO-IMAGEGENERATION

**中文:**

#### A.3.2 文本到图像生成

---

<span id="S015-03"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-03

**Original:**

We train our model on the LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ dataset, using the text prompt portion as training data. To enable efficient
fine-tuning, we employ Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with rank r= 64 and LoRA alpha α= 64 . In the SANA 1.6B
experiments, the auxiliary model is therefore not a full-parameter copy of the teacher: only approximately 34M LoRA
parameters are trainable, which is less than 2.2% of the 1.6B-parameter backbone. We use the 8-bit Adam optimizer
(AdamW8bit) with its default parameters for memory-efficient training. The learning rate is set to 1×10 −4 for both the
auxiliary flow modelv ϕ and the student generatorG θ, without any learning rate scheduling or warmup.

**中文:**

我们在 LAION-aesthetic-6.5+ 数据集上训练模型，使用其中的文本提示部分作为训练数据。为实现高效微调，我们采用低秩适配（LoRA），秩 $r = 64$、LoRA alpha $\alpha = 64$。因此在 SANA 1.6B 实验中，辅助模型并不是教师的全参数副本：只有约 34M 个 LoRA 参数可训练，不到 1.6B 参数主干的 2.2%。我们使用 8 位 Adam 优化器（AdamW8bit）及其默认参数以实现内存高效的训练。辅助流模型 $v_\phi$ 与学生生成器 $G_\theta$ 的学习率都设为 $1\times10^{-4}$，不使用任何学习率调度或预热。

---

<span id="S015-04"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-04

**Original:**

Training is conducted on 1 NVIDIA PRO 6000 GPU, with a batch size of 1 per GPU and no gradient accumulation. We
enable mixed-precision training using BF16 to accelerate computation and reduce memory consumption while maintaining
numerical stability. Gradient clipping with a maximum norm of 0.5 is applied to prevent gradient explosion. The output
image resolution is set to1024×1024pixels to generate high-fidelity images.

**中文:**

训练在 1 块 NVIDIA PRO 6000 GPU 上进行，每 GPU 批大小为 1，且不做梯度累积。我们启用 BF16 混合精度训练以加速计算并降低内存消耗，同时保持数值稳定性。施加最大范数为 0.5 的梯度裁剪以防止梯度爆炸。输出图像分辨率设为 $1024\times1024$ 像素以生成高保真图像。

---

<span id="S015-05"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-05

**Original:**

For timestep sampling, we adopt a logit-normal biased sampling strategy similar to the 4D occupancy forecasting experiments,
which allocates more sampling density to intermediate timesteps where the velocity field exhibits higher variance. When
computing the mean flow, we employ an ODE solver with step size∆τ= 0.03 . The student model is configured for 4-step
inference during evaluation. During distillation, the teacher model uQ is evaluated with classifier-free guidance (CFG) at a
guidance scale of w= 4.5 to provide strong conditional generation signals, while the student model operates without CFG
to directly model the target distribution. We train for a total of 3000 optimization steps and use the final checkpoint for
evaluation.

**中文:**

在时间步采样方面，我们采用与 4D 占据预测实验类似的 logit-normal 偏置采样策略，把更多采样密度分配给速度场方差更高的中间时间步。在计算均值流时，我们采用步长 $\Delta\tau = 0.03$ 的 ODE 求解器。评估时学生模型配置为 4 步推理。在蒸馏过程中，教师模型 $u_Q$ 在无分类器引导（CFG）、引导尺度 $w = 4.5$ 下评估，以提供强条件生成信号，而学生模型在没有 CFG 的情况下运行以直接建模目标分布。我们共训练 3000 个优化步，并使用最终检查点进行评估。

---

<span id="S015-06"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-06

**Original:**

B. Discussion

**中文:**

## B 讨论

---

<span id="S015-07"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-07

**Original:**

B.1. Relationship to Align Your Flow and Mean Flow

**中文:**

### B.1 与 Align Your Flow 及均值流的关系

---

<span id="S015-08"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-08

**Original:**

MFD is closely related to recent flow-model acceleration methods, but it occupies a different optimization regime. Align
Your Flow (AYF) (Sabour et al., 2025) is a consistency distillation method: it enforces that points along a teacher ODE
trajectory map to the same terminal sample. MFD instead performs distribution matching in velocity space by aligning
expected average velocity fields. Thus, AYF imposes sample-level flow-map consistency, while MFD imposes distribution-
level mean-flow consistency. This distinction matters early in distillation, when the student distribution can be far from the
teacher: exact sample-level constraints can be overly restrictive, whereas expected mean-flow matching tolerates imperfect
intermediate samples while still guaranteeingP=Qat convergence under Theorem 4.3.

**中文:**

MFD 与近期流模型加速方法密切相关，但处于不同的优化机制区间。Align Your Flow（AYF）（Sabour et al., 2025）是一种一致性蒸馏方法：它强制沿教师 ODE 轨迹的点映射到同一终端样本。而 MFD 通过在速度空间中对齐期望平均速度场来实现分布匹配。因此，AYF 施加的是样本级流映射一致性，而 MFD 施加的是分布级均值流一致性。这一区别在蒸馏早期、学生分布可能远离教师时很重要：严格的样本级约束可能过于苛刻，而期望均值流匹配能够容忍不完美的中间样本，同时在定理 4.3 下仍保证收敛时 $P=Q$。

---

<span id="S015-09"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-09

**Original:**

Mean Flow (MF) (Geng et al., 2025a) shares the idea of supervising time-integrated velocities, but the setting and objective
are different. MF trains a generative flow model from scratch using data-derived mean velocity targets. MFD distills an
already pretrained flow matching teacher into a single-step or few-step generator, and introduces an auxiliary flow model
to estimate the velocity field induced by the evolving student distribution. Consequently, MFD is intended as a general
acceleration mechanism for existing high-quality FM models rather than a replacement for full generative training.

**中文:**

均值流（MF）（Geng et al., 2025a）也采用以时间积分速度进行监督的思路，但其设置与目标不同。MF 使用数据导出的平均速度目标从零训练生成式流模型。MFD 则把已预训练的 Flow Matching 教师蒸馏为单步或少步生成器，并引入辅助流模型来估计演化中学生分布所诱导的速度场。因此，MFD 旨在作为现有高质量 FM 模型的通用加速机制，而非替代完整的生成式训练。

---

<span id="S015-10"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-10

**Original:**

B.2. The difference between Mean Flow Distillation and Rectified Reflow

**中文:**

### B.2 均值流蒸馏与整流重流的区别

---

<span id="S015-11"></span>
**Source:** p.15 S015-11

**Original:**

While 2-Rectified Flow (Reflow) (Liu et al., 2023) and Mean Flow Distillation (MFD) share the overarching goal of
accelerating sampling, they represent fundamentally different approaches to the problem of flow simplification. In this

**中文:**

尽管 2-整流流（Reflow）（Liu et al., 2023）与均值流蒸馏（MFD）在加速采样这一总体目标上一致，但它们代表了流简化问题中根本不同的思路。在本

<span id="S016-02"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-02

**Original:**

section, we argue that Reflow and MFD operate in distinct regimes. Reflow is a trajectory regularization technique, whereas
MFD is a distribution alignment framework. We provide a detailed analysis of why the instantaneous supervision in Reflow
remains insufficient for high-fidelity one-step generation on complex manifolds, and how MFD addresses this through
temporal integration.

**中文:**

（承接上页）节中，我们论证 Reflow 与 MFD 运行于不同的机制区间。Reflow 是一种轨迹正则化技术，而 MFD 是一个分布对齐框架。我们详细分析为何 Reflow 中的瞬时监督仍不足以在复杂流形上实现高保真单步生成，以及 MFD 如何通过时间积分解决这一问题。

#### B.2.1 优化动力学

The most important difference lies in what is smoothed during optimization.

Reflow Smoothes the Target (Label). The objective of Reflow is to regress the student’s instantaneous velocityvθ(Xt, t)
toward the straight path connecting data pairs(X 0, X1):

**中文:**

**Reflow 平滑的是目标（标签）。** Reflow 的目标是把学生的瞬时速度 $v_\theta(X_t, t)$ 回归到连接数据对 $(X_0, X_1)$ 的直线路径：

Here, the target Y= (X 1 −X 0) is indeed a low-variance, “straightened” signal because it represents the integral of the
teacher’s trajectory,
R 1

this loss with respect to parametersθdepends directly on the student’s instantaneous output:

**中文:**

这里目标 $Y = (X_1 - X_0)$ 确实是一个低方差、“被拉直”的信号，因为它表示教师轨迹的积分 $\int_0^1 u_{\mathrm{teacher}}\,d\tau$。这有效地平均掉了教师流的曲率。然而，该损失对参数 $\theta$ 的梯度直接依赖学生的瞬时输出：

∇θLReflow ∝(v θ(Xt, t)−Y)· ∇ θvθ(Xt, t).(22)

$$\nabla_\theta \mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{Reflow}} \propto (v_\theta(X_t, t) - Y)\cdot\nabla_\theta v_\theta(X_t, t). \tag{22}$$

---

<span id="S016-09"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-09

Crucially, if the student network vθ exhibits high variance or stochasticity at specific timesteps t due to mini-batch noise or
optimization instability, this noise propagates directly into the gradient update. Smoothing the target Y does not mitigate the
noise inherent in the estimatorv θ(Xt, t).

**中文:**

关键在于，如果学生网络 $v_\theta$ 由于小批量噪声或优化不稳定而在某些时间步 $t$ 表现出高方差或随机性，这种噪声会直接传播到梯度更新中。平滑目标 $Y$ 并不能减轻估计器 $v_\theta(X_t, t)$ 固有的噪声。

---

<span id="S016-10"></span>

MFD Smoothes the Estimator (Gradient). In contrast, MFD defines the loss over the integrated output of the auxiliary
student flowv P :

$$\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{MFD}} \propto \Big\|\int_s^t v_P(X_\tau, \tau)\,d\tau - \int_s^t u_Q(X_\tau, \tau)\,d\tau\Big\|^2. \tag{23}$$

<span id="S016-12"></span>

**Original:**

From a signal processing perspective, the integration operator I(·) =
R t
s (·)dτ acts as a temporal low-pass filter on the
student’s vector field. If we model the student’s instantaneous estimation as the true signal plus zero-mean optimization
noise, ˆv=v+ξ , the Reflow gradient variance is bounded by Var(ξ). The MFD gradient variance, however, is determined
by the variance of the integral ofξ.

**中文:**

从信号处理视角看，积分算子 $I(\cdot) = \int_s^t (\cdot)\,d\tau$ 对学生的向量场起时间低通滤波器的作用。若把学生的瞬时估计建模为真实信号加零均值优化噪声，即 $\hat{v} = v + \xi$，则 Reflow 的梯度方差以 $\mathrm{Var}(\xi)$ 为界；而 MFD 的梯度方差由 $\xi$ 的积分的方差决定。

---

**Original:**

As derived in our theoretical analysis (Appendix C.3), for a discrete approximation withKsteps, the variance scales as:

**中文:**

正如我们的理论分析（附录 C.3）所推导，对于 $K$ 步的离散近似，方差按如下方式缩放：

Var(∇LMFD)≈ 1
