---

<span id="S016-15"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-15

**Original:**

This establishes MFD not merely as an alternative, but as a strictly more stable optimization objective. On complex tasks
like 4D occupancy forecasting, where the manifold topology induces high-frequency fluctuations in velocity fields, this
factor of1/Kvariance reduction is the difference between convergence and mode collapse.

**中文:**

这确立 MFD 不仅是一种替代方案，而是一个严格更稳定的优化目标。在 4D 占据预测这样流形拓扑会在速度场中诱导高频波动的复杂任务上，这一 $1/K$ 的方差降低因子正是收敛与模式崩塌之间的差别。

---

<span id="S016-16"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-16

**Original:**

B.2.2. GEOMETRICINTERPRETATION

**中文:**

#### B.2.2 几何解释

---

<span id="S016-17"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-17

**Original:**

Reflow and MFD enforce different geometric constraints on the generative mapping.

**中文:**

Reflow 与 MFD 对生成映射施加不同的几何约束。

---

<span id="S016-18"></span>
**Source:** p.16 S016-18

**Original:**

Reflow assumes that the optimal transport map can be well-approximated by a velocity field that is constant in time
(v=const ). While theoretically possible (Monge-Amp`ere transportation), in practice, learning a perfectly straight flow on
high-dimensional, multimodal data is challenging. Residual curvature often remains. Consequently, sampling Reflow with a
single step (N F E= 1 ) is an approximation: X1 ≈X 0 +v(X 0). Any deviation from straightness results in discretization
error (truncation error), leading to blurry or artifact-heavy samples (as observed in Table 1 of the main text).

**中文:**

Reflow 假设最优传输映射可以被一个时间上恒定的速度场（$v = \mathrm{const}$）很好地近似。虽然这在理论上可行（Monge-Ampère 传输），但在实践中，在高维多模态数据上学习完全笔直的流是困难的。残余曲率往往依然存在。因此，用单步采样 Reflow（$\mathrm{NFE}=1$）只是一种近似：$X_1 \approx X_0 + v(X_0)$。任何偏离笔直性的情况都会导致离散化误差（截断误差），从而产生模糊或伪影严重的样本（如正文表 1 中所观察到的）。

---

[第 16 页](#page-16)

<span id="page-17"></span>
## PDF 第 17 页

<span id="S017-01"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-01

**Original:**

MFD does not inherently force the auxiliary flow to be straight. Instead, it encourages the student generator Gθ (a one-step
mapper) to land on the correct distribution Q, as a relaxed target. The auxiliary model vP and the integral formulation serve
as a bridge. By matching the Mean Flow, we enforce:

**中文:**

MFD 并不强制辅助流为直线。相反，它鼓励学生生成器 $G_\theta$（一个单步映射器）落到正确的分布 $Q$ 上，把它作为一个松弛目标。辅助模型 $v_P$ 与积分形式则充当桥梁。通过匹配 Mean Flow，我们强制：

---

<span id="S017-02"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-02

**Original:**

X0 +
Z 1
0
vP dτ
| {z }
Student Endpoint
=X 0 +
Z 1
0
uQdτ
| {z }
Teacher Endpoint
.(25)

**中文:**

$$X_0 + \underbrace{\int_0^1 v_P\,d\tau}_{\text{学生端点}} = X_0 + \underbrace{\int_0^1 u_Q\,d\tau}_{\text{教师端点}}. \tag{25}$$

> PDF 抽取把该公式拆成多行，且下括号标注（Student Endpoint / Teacher Endpoint）单独成行；此处按语义重建。

---

<span id="S017-03"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-03

**Original:**

This ensures that the destination matches the teacher, regardless of the intermediate path complexity. MFD creates a student
Gθ that is explicitly trained to minimize the error of the final sample X1, whereas Reflow minimizes the error of the vector
field vt. For one-step generation, minimizing endpoint error (via MFD) is the more direct and theoretically sound objective.

**中文:**

这确保目标点与教师一致，而不论中间路径多么复杂。MFD 训练出的学生模型 $G_\theta$ 被显式训练以最小化最终样本 $X_1$ 的误差，而 Reflow 最小化的是向量场 $v_t$ 的误差。对于单步生成，最小化端点误差（通过 MFD）是更直接且在理论上更合理的目标。

---

<span id="S017-04"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-04

**Original:**

B.2.3. JUSTIFICATION FOR THEAUXILIARYMODEL

**中文:**

### B.2.3 辅助模型的合理性论证

---

<span id="S017-05"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-05

**Original:**

A potential critique of MFD is the extra computational overhead of training an auxiliary flow model vP . We argue this cost
is justified and necessary for three reasons:

**中文:**

对 MFD 的一个潜在批评是训练辅助流模型 $v_P$ 带来的额外计算开销。我们认为这一代价是合理且必要的，理由有三：

---

<span id="S017-06"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-06

**Original:**

1. Decoupling Generation and Estimation. The student generator Gθ is a direct X0 →X 1 mapper. It does not possess a
time-dependent vector field structure required for ODE-based comparison. The auxiliary model vP acts as a variational
proxy, reconstructing the vector field implied by Gθ. This allows us to leverage the robust mathematical framework of
Flow Matching to train a model that does not output vector fields (the Student).

**中文:**

**1. 解耦生成与估计。** 学生生成器 $G_\theta$ 是一个直接的 $X_0 \to X_1$ 映射器，不具备基于 ODE 比较所需的时间相关向量场结构。辅助模型 $v_P$ 充当变分代理，重建 $G_\theta$ 所隐含的向量场。这使我们能够利用 Flow Matching 稳健的数学框架，来训练一个不输出向量场的模型（即学生模型）。

---

<span id="S017-07"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-07

**Original:**

2. Zero Inference Overhead. It is crucial to note that the auxiliary model vP is strictly a training-time artifact. During
inference, only Gθ is used. Thus, MFD achieves the inference efficiency of a GAN (1 step) with the training stability
of Flow Matching.

**中文:**

**2. 零推理开销。** 需要强调的是，辅助模型 $v_P$ 严格来说是训练期产物。推理时只使用 $G_\theta$。因此，MFD 在获得 Flow Matching 训练稳定性的同时，达到了 GAN 的推理效率（1 步）。

---

<span id="S017-08"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-08

**Original:**

3. Handling Complex Topologies. In our experiments on LiDAR-based 4D occupancy, we observed that direct distillation
methods failed to capture the discrete-continuous nature of the data. The auxiliary model provides the necessary
capacity to approximate the mean velocity of these complex transitions, preventing the “averaging” artifacts common
in simpler regression objectives. We give a mathematical proof of this in Section C.4.

**中文:**

**3. 处理复杂拓扑。** 在基于 LiDAR 的 4D 占据预测实验中，我们观察到直接蒸馏方法无法捕捉数据的离散—连续特性。辅助模型提供了必要的容量来近似这些复杂转移的平均速度，从而避免更简单的回归目标中常见的“平均化”伪影。我们在 C.4 节给出该结论的数学证明。

---

<span id="S017-09"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-09

**Original:**

B.3. A Signal Processing Perspective on Flow Distillation

**中文:**

### B.3 从信号处理视角看流蒸馏

---

<span id="S017-10"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-10

**Original:**

In this section, we re-examine Mean Flow Distillation through the perspective of signal processing, formally characterizing
the proposed method as a temporal low-pass filter applied to the supervision signal. We posit that this spectral filtering
property is the primary driver of the observed training stability and variance reduction.

**中文:**

本节从信号处理视角重新审视 Mean Flow Distillation，把所提方法形式化为施加于监督信号上的时间低通滤波器。我们提出，这种频谱滤波特性是所观察到的训练稳定性与方差降低的主要驱动因素。

---

<span id="S017-11"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-11

**Original:**

In the context of distillation, the student model Gθ attempts to align its induced flow with that of a pre-trained teacher uQ.
However, the teacher’s instantaneous velocity fielduQ(Xt, t) often serves as a noisy proxy for the ideal transport dynamics
due to optimization stochasticity and high-frequency curvature in the learned manifold.

**中文:**

在蒸馏语境下，学生模型 $G_\theta$ 试图使其诱导的流与预训练教师 $u_Q$ 的流对齐。然而，由于优化随机性以及所学流形中的高频曲率，教师的瞬时速度场 $u_Q(X_t,t)$ 往往只是理想传输动力学的含噪代理。

---

<span id="S017-12"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-12

**Original:**

We define the supervision signalS(t)at a trajectory pointX t as a superposition of the true underlying transport semantics
v∗(t)(the signal) and a stochastic error termξ(t)(the noise):

**中文:**

我们把轨迹点 $X_t$ 处的监督信号 $S(t)$ 定义为真实底层传输语义 $v^*(t)$（信号）与随机误差项 $\xi(t)$（噪声）的叠加：

---

<span id="S017-13"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-13

**Original:**

S(t) =u Q(Xt, t) =v ∗(t) +ξ(t),(26)

**中文:**

$$S(t) = u_Q(X_t,t) = v^*(t) + \xi(t), \tag{26}$$

---

<span id="S017-14"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-14

**Original:**

where ξ(t) represents high-frequency oscillatory components arising from approximation errors or local geometric perturba-
tions. In standard Instantaneous Velocity Field Matching (analogous to VSD), the optimization objective LIVF forces the
student to minimize the discrepancy at every instantt:

**中文:**

其中 $\xi(t)$ 表示由近似误差或局部几何扰动引起的高频振荡分量。在标准的瞬时速度场匹配（类似 VSD）中，优化目标 $L_{IVF}$ 迫使学生在每个时刻 $t$ 最小化差异：

**Source:** p.17 S017-15

**Original:**

**中文:**

$$\min_\theta \; \mathbb{E}_t\left[\left\|v_P(X_t,t) - \left(v^*(t) + \xi(t)\right)\right\|_2^2\right]. \tag{27}$$

> 抽取文本把该公式拆为 min / θ / E_t / 方括号 / 范数等 9 行，且方括号被编码为控制字符 U+0002、U+0003；此处已还原为 [、] 并按语义重建为单行公式。

---

<span id="S017-16"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-16

**Original:**

From a signal processing standpoint, this objective functions as an all-pass filter. The student is penalized for failing to
reproduce the noise term ξ(t), leading to high-variance gradients and overfitting to local irregularities that are irrelevant to
the global transport map.

**中文:**

从信号处理的角度看，该目标相当于一个全通滤波器（all-pass filter）。学生会因未能复现噪声项 $\xi(t)$ 而受到惩罚，从而导致高方差梯度，并对与全局传输映射无关的局部不规则性过拟合。

---

<span id="S017-17"></span>
**Source:** p.17 S017-17

**Original:**

MFD fundamentally alters this dynamic by shifting the alignment target from the instantaneous velocity to the Average
Velocity Field (A VF). Recall our definition of the A VF over the interval[s, t]:

**中文:**

MFD 通过把对齐目标从瞬时速度转移到平均速度场（Average Velocity Field，AVF），从根本上改变了这一动态。回顾我们对区间 $[s,t]$ 上 AVF 的定义：

**中文:**

$$U(X_s,s,t) = \frac{1}{t-s}\int_s^t u_Q(\psi_\tau(X_0),\tau)\,d\tau. \tag{28}$$

$$U(X_s,s,t) = \underbrace{\frac{1}{\Delta t}\int_s^t v^*(\tau)\,d\tau}_{\text{平滑信号}} + \underbrace{\frac{1}{\Delta t}\int_s^t \xi(\tau)\,d\tau}_{\text{部分滤除的噪声}}, \tag{29}$$

<span id="S018-03"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-03

**Original:**

where ∆t=t−s . The operation 1
∆t
R t
s (·)dτ corresponds to a temporal boxcar filter (or moving average). In the frequency
domain, the magnitude response of such a filter is given by the sinc function|sinc(f∆t)|, which decays as1/f. Consequently,
the integration operator acts as a low-pass filter, significantly attenuating the high-frequency noise components in ξ(t) while
preserving the low-frequency trends of the transport trajectoryv ∗(t).

**中文:**

其中 $\Delta t = t-s$。操作 $\frac{1}{\Delta t}\int_s^t(\cdot)\,d\tau$ 对应时间上的矩形窗（boxcar）滤波器（即滑动平均）。在频域中，该滤波器的幅度响应由 sinc 函数 $|\mathrm{sinc}(f\Delta t)|$ 给出，其随 $1/f$ 衰减。因此，积分算子起到低通滤波器的作用，显著衰减 $\xi(t)$ 中的高频噪声分量，同时保留传输轨迹 $v^*(t)$ 的低频趋势。

---

<span id="S018-04"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-04

**Original:**

This spectral interpretation explains why MFD is robust to local perturbations. By supervising the student with the integral
of the velocity field—effectively the displacement vector—we enforce global trajectory consistency (phase alignment) rather
than local tangent alignment. The student learns to match the global displacement rather than the volatile instantaneous
velocity.

**中文:**

这一频谱解释说明了 MFD 为何对局部扰动具有鲁棒性。通过以速度场的积分——实际上即位移向量——来监督学生，我们强制的是全局轨迹一致性（相位对齐），而非局部切向对齐。学生学会匹配全局位移，而不是易变的瞬时速度。

---

<span id="S018-05"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-05

**Original:**

B.4. Future Research Directions

**中文:**

### B.4 未来研究方向

---

<span id="S018-06"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-06

**Original:**

The success of Mean Flow Distillation (MFD) suggests a fundamental shift in how we approach generative model accelera-
tion: moving from instantaneous velocity matching to time-integrated mean flow alignment significantly reduces gradient
variance and improves training stability. This integral-based supervision effectively functions as a temporal low-pass filter
against optimization noise, opening the door to multi-scale temporal distillation strategies. From a geometric perspective,
MFD’s emphasis on temporal trajectory implies that preserving the properties of the transport path is more critical than
precise pointwise alignment. This insight paves the way for extending flow distillation to non-Euclidean manifolds or
the spectral domain, where explicit trajectory regularization can better handle complex data topologies. Ultimately, MFD
demonstrates that robust distillation requires looking beyond the teacher’s instantaneous output to capture the cumulative
dynamics of where the teacher leads over time.

**中文:**

Mean Flow Distillation（MFD）的成功表明，生成模型加速方法需要一次根本转变：从瞬时速度匹配转向时间积分的平均流对齐，可显著降低梯度方差并提升训练稳定性。这种基于积分的监督实际上起到了针对优化噪声的时间低通滤波作用，为多尺度时间蒸馏策略打开了大门。从几何角度看，MFD 对时间轨迹的强调意味着保持传输路径的性质比精确的逐点对齐更为关键。这一洞见为把流蒸馏推广到非欧几里得流形或频谱域铺平了道路——在这些场景中，显式的轨迹正则化能更好地处理复杂的数据拓扑。最终，MFD 表明稳健的蒸馏需要超越教师的瞬时输出，去捕捉教师随时间所引导的累积动力学。

---

<span id="S018-07"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-07

**Original:**

B.5. Discussion on Error Accumulation in Temporal Integration

**中文:**

### B.5 关于时间积分中误差累积的讨论

---

<span id="S018-08"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-08

**Original:**

One intuitive perspective on why Mean Flow Distillation provides stronger supervision than instantaneous velocity matching
is the error accumulation effect during temporal integration. This viewpoint suggests that if the student’s velocity field
deviates from the teacher’s at any point along a trajectory, these local deviations compound when integrated over the time
interval [s, t], resulting in larger discrepancies in the average velocity field. In this sense, temporal integration acts as an
amplification mechanism that makes even small instantaneous velocity errors more detectable and correctable.

**中文:**

一个直观视角可以解释为何 Mean Flow Distillation 比瞬时速度匹配提供更强的监督：时间积分中的误差累积效应。该观点认为，如果学生的速度场在轨迹上任一点偏离教师，这些局部偏差在区间 $[s,t]$ 上积分后会被叠加，从而在平均速度场中产生更大的差异。在这个意义上，时间积分充当放大机制，使即便很小的瞬时速度误差也更容易被检测并纠正。

---

<span id="S018-09"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-09

**Original:**

Consider a particle moving along a flow trajectory. If at each infinitesimal time stepdτ, the student’s velocityvP (Xτ , τ)
differs from the teacher’s velocity uQ(Xτ , τ) by a small amount δv(τ) , then over the interval [s, t], the cumulative
displacement error becomes:

**中文:**

考虑一个沿流轨迹运动的粒子。若在每个无穷小时间步 $d\tau$ 上，学生的速度 $v_P(X_\tau,\tau)$ 与教师的速度 $u_Q(X_\tau,\tau)$ 相差一个小量 $\delta v(\tau)$，则在区间 $[s,t]$ 上累积的位移误差为：

---

<span id="S018-10"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-10

**Original:**

∆cumulative =
Z t
s
δv(τ)dτ=
Z t
s
(
vP (Xτ , τ)−u Q(Xτ , τ)
)
dτ(30)

**中文:**

$$\Delta_{\text{cumulative}} = \int_s^t \delta v(\tau)\,d\tau = \int_s^t \left(v_P(X_\tau,\tau) - u_Q(X_\tau,\tau)\right)d\tau. \tag{30}$$

> 公式中的大圆括号在抽取文本中丢失（开括号成为空行，闭括号被编码为控制字符 U+0001）；此处按语义还原为 ( 与 )。

---

<span id="S018-11"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-11

**Original:**

This cumulative error is reflected in the mean velocity discrepancy:

**中文:**

该累积误差体现在平均速度的差异中：

---

<span id="S018-12"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-12

**Original:**

U P (Xs, s, t)−U Q(Xs, s, t) = 1
t−s
Z t

vP (Xτ , τ)−u Q(Xτ , τ)
)
dτ(31)

**中文:**

$$U_P(X_s,s,t) - U_Q(X_s,s,t) = \frac{1}{t-s}\int_s^t \left(v_P(X_\tau,\tau) - u_Q(X_\tau,\tau)\right)d\tau. \tag{31}$$

> 公式中的大圆括号在抽取文本中丢失（开括号成为空行，闭括号被编码为控制字符 U+0001）；此处按语义还原为 ( 与 )。

---

<span id="S018-13"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-13

**Original:**

From this perspective, the temporal integration converts pointwise errors into integrated displacement errors, which could be
viewed as providing a stronger supervision signal that accumulates information about the velocity field mismatch over the
entire time interval rather than at a single instant.

**中文:**

从这个角度看，时间积分把逐点误差转换为积分位移误差，因而可被视为提供了更强的监督信号：它在整个时间区间上、而非单个时刻上累积关于速度场失配的信息。

---

<span id="S018-14"></span>
**Source:** p.18 S018-14

**Original:**

Relationship to Variance Reduction.While this error accumulation perspective offers an intuitive explanation for why
matching mean velocities might be more effective than matching instantaneous velocities, it is important to note that this
viewpoint focuses on the magnitude of the supervision signal under the assumption that the student model systematically
deviates from the teacher. In practice, the primary challenge in distillation is not necessarily the magnitude of the error
signal, but rather the variance and noise in gradient estimation caused by stochastic factors such as mini-batch sampling,
limited model capacity, and numerical approximation errors.

**中文:**

**与方差降低的关系。** 尽管这一误差累积视角为“匹配平均速度为何可能比匹配瞬时速度更有效”提供了直观解释，但需要注意的是，该视角关注的是在学生模型系统性偏离教师这一假设下监督信号的幅度。在实践中，蒸馏的主要挑战未必是误差信号的幅度，而是由小批量采样、模型容量受限与数值近似误差等随机因素所引起的梯度估计的方差与噪声。

---

<span id="page-19"></span>
## PDF 第 19 页

<span id="S019-C1"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-C1

**Original:**

Figure 3.Comparison of gradient differences when computing vs. omitting the Jacobian term during backpropagation to Xs over 10,000

Direction difference histogram showing the cosine similarity between normalized gradient vectors. The results reveal that while gradient

**中文:**

**图 3.** 在 10,000 个训练步上，对 $X_s$ 反向传播时计算与省略 Jacobian 项的梯度差异比较。左：长度差异直方图，显示梯度幅值的绝对差异 $\big|\|\nabla^{full}_{X_s}\| - \|\nabla^{approx}_{X_s}\|\big|$。右：方向差异直方图，显示归一化梯度向量之间的余弦相似度。结果表明，尽管梯度幅值保持相近，但在包含完整 Jacobian 计算时方向显著偏离。

> 图内标注的上下标（∇^{full}_{X_s}、∇^{approx}_{X_s}）在抽取文本中被压平为两行；公式按语义还原。插图本体不嵌入。

---

<span id="S019-02"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-02

**Original:**

Our theoretical analysis in Section C.3 demonstrates that the key advantage of MFD lies in its variance reduction property:
by averaging instantaneous velocity estimates over multiple integration steps, the temporal integration acts as a low-pass
filter that suppresses high-frequency optimization noise, yielding gradient estimates with variance scaling as 1
K (where K
is the number of integration steps) compared to instantaneous matching. This variance reduction is the mathematically
rigorous mechanism underlying MFD’s improved training stability and convergence.

**中文:**

我们在 C.3 节的理论分析表明，MFD 的关键优势在于其方差降低性质：通过在多个积分步上平均瞬时速度估计，时间积分起到低通滤波器的作用，抑制高频优化噪声，使梯度估计的方差相对于瞬时匹配按 $1/K$ 缩放（$K$ 为积分步数）。这种方差降低是 MFD 训练稳定性与收敛性提升背后的数学严格机制。

---

<span id="S019-03"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-03

**Original:**

In summary, the error accumulation and variance reduction perspectives are complementary rather than contradictory:

**中文:**

总之，误差累积与方差降低两种视角是互补的，而非相互矛盾：

---

<span id="S019-04"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-04

**Original:**

• Error accumulationprovides an intuitive geometric interpretation: temporal integration converts local velocity
mismatches into global displacement errors, potentially making the supervision signal more informative about trajectory-
level alignment.
• Variance reductionprovides a rigorous statistical analysis: temporal integration averages out stochastic noise across
multiple time steps, yielding more stable and reliable gradient estimates that facilitate convergent optimization.

**中文:**

- **误差累积**提供了直观的几何解释：时间积分把局部速度失配转换为全局位移误差，可能使监督信号对轨迹级对齐更具信息量。
- **方差降低**提供了严格的统计分析：时间积分在多个时间步上平均随机噪声，得到更稳定、更可靠的梯度估计，从而促进收敛的优化。

---

<span id="S019-05"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-05

**Original:**

While we find the error accumulation viewpoint conceptually appealing, we have not yet identified a suitable mathematical
or experimental framework to rigorously validate and quantify this effect independently of the variance reduction mechanism.
Future work could explore decomposing these two effects—perhaps through carefully controlled synthetic experiments
where the noise level and systematic bias of velocity field estimates can be independently manipulated—to better understand
their relative contributions to MFD’s effectiveness.

**中文:**

虽然我们认为误差累积视角在概念上有吸引力，但我们尚未找到合适的数学或实验框架，能够在独立于方差降低机制的情况下严格验证并量化该效应。未来的工作可以探索将这两种效应分解——也许可以通过精心控制的合成实验，在其中独立操纵速度场估计的噪声水平与系统性偏差——从而更好地理解它们对 MFD 有效性的相对贡献。

---

<span id="S019-06"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-06

**Original:**

B.6. Discussion on the rationality of omitting the Jacobian term

**中文:**

### B.6 关于省略 Jacobian 项合理性的讨论

---

<span id="S019-07"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-07

**Original:**

In the derivation of Mean Flow Distillation, when computing the gradient with respect to the student generator parameters θ,
the exact mathematical formulation would involve backpropagating through the ODE integration process, which theoretically
requires computing the Jacobian matrices of both the auxiliary flow model vϕ and the teacher model uQ. However, in our
practical implementation, we treat these Jacobian terms as identity matrices, effectively stopping the gradient flow through
the auxiliary and teacher models when computing ∇θLM F D. This approximation, while mathematically inexact, is critical
for achieving stable and high-quality distillation results.

**中文:**

在 Mean Flow Distillation 的推导中，当计算关于学生生成器参数 $\theta$ 的梯度时，精确的数学表述需要沿 ODE 积分过程反向传播，这在理论上需要计算辅助流模型 $v_\phi$ 与教师模型 $u_Q$ 二者的 Jacobian 矩阵。然而在实际实现中，我们在计算 $\nabla_\theta L_{MFD}$ 时将这些 Jacobian 项视为单位矩阵，从而有效地切断了通过辅助模型与教师模型的梯度流。这一近似在数学上并不精确，但对于获得稳定且高质量的蒸馏结果至关重要。

---

<span id="S019-08"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-08

**Original:**

To empirically investigate the impact of this approximation, we conducted a comparative analysis during the text-to-image
generation experiments (SANA 1.6B (Xie et al., 2025)) over the first 10,000 training steps. We computed the gradients with
respect to the intermediate variableX s under two scenarios: (1) with full Jacobian computation (backpropagating through
both auxiliary and teacher models), and (2) with the identity matrix approximation (stopping gradients at the auxiliary
and teacher models). Figure 3 presents histograms comparing these two gradient computation strategies along two critical
dimensions.

**中文:**

为实证研究该近似的影响，我们在文本到图像生成实验（SANA 1.6B (Xie et al., 2025)）的前 10,000 个训练步上进行了对比分析。我们在两种情形下计算关于中间变量 $X_s$ 的梯度：(1) 完整计算 Jacobian（对辅助模型与教师模型均反向传播）；(2) 单位矩阵近似（在辅助模型与教师模型处停止梯度）。图 3 展示了沿两个关键维度比较这两种梯度计算策略的直方图。

---

<span id="S019-09"></span>
**Source:** p.19 S019-09

**Original:**

The left panel of Figure 3 shows the length difference histogram, which measures the absolute difference between the L2

**中文:**

图 3 的左图给出长度差异直方图，它度量两个梯度向量 L2

## PDF 第 20 页

<span id="S020-01"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-01

**Original:**

norms of the two gradient vectors: |∥∇full
Xs ∥ − ∥∇approx
Xs ∥|. This metric evaluates whether the gradient magnitudes remain
consistent across the two computation strategies. The right panel shows the direction difference histogram, which computes
the cosine similarity between the normalized gradient vectors, providing a measure of directional alignment in the range
[−1,1], where values close to 1 indicate strong directional agreement.

**中文:**

两个梯度向量的 L2 范数：$\big|\|\nabla^{full}_{X_s}\| - \|\nabla^{approx}_{X_s}\|\big|$。该指标评估两种计算策略下的梯度幅值是否保持一致。右图给出方向差异直方图，它计算归一化梯度向量之间的余弦相似度，从而给出范围在 $[-1,1]$ 内的方向对齐度量：数值接近 1 表示方向高度一致。

> 本块续接第 19 页末未完成的句子；图内符号上下标在抽取文本中被压平，此处按语义还原。

---

<span id="S020-02"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-02

**Original:**

The empirical results reveal a striking phenomenon: while the gradient magnitudes remain relatively consistent (left panel
shows most differences concentrated near zero), the gradient directions diverge dramatically (right panel shows a broad
distribution with many samples exhibiting low or even negative cosine similarity). This directional inconsistency explains
why training with full Jacobian computation fails to produce reasonable, high-quality results in our experiments. We
hypothesize that this directional distortion arises because backpropagating through both the auxiliary flow model and the
teacher model introduces substantial noise and complex higher-order dependencies that corrupt the gradient signal. The ODE
integration process involves multiple sequential function evaluations, and backpropagating through this entire computational
graph amplifies approximation errors and numerical instabilities, ultimately distorting the gradient direction away from the
true descent direction toward the optimal student distribution.

**中文:**

实证结果揭示了一个显著现象：尽管梯度幅值保持相对一致（左图显示多数差异集中在零附近），梯度方向却剧烈偏离（右图呈现宽分布，许多样本的余弦相似度很低甚至为负）。这种方向不一致解释了为何在我们的实验中，使用完整 Jacobian 计算训练无法产生合理且高质量的结果。我们假设这种方向畸变源于：同时通过辅助流模型与教师模型反向传播会引入大量噪声和复杂的高阶依赖，从而污染梯度信号。ODE 积分过程包含多次顺序的函数评估，沿整个计算图反向传播会放大近似误差与数值不稳定性，最终使梯度方向偏离真实下降方向、远离最优的学生分布。

---

<span id="S020-03"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-03

**Original:**

In contrast, the identity matrix approximation, stopping gradients at the auxiliary and teacher models, provides a cleaner,
more direct supervision signal. This approximation aligns with the motivation behind score distillation methods: the teacher
model serves as a fixed evaluator that provides supervision signals, rather than a differentiable component of the student’s
training objective. By treating the mean flow computed from the teacher and auxiliary models as a fixed target, we avoid the
gradient distortion caused by backpropagating through complex, potentially ill-conditioned neural ODEs. This pragmatic
approximation strategy has precedent in the distillation literature. Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) (Poole et al., 2023) and
its variants similarly stop gradients through the teacher diffusion model, treating the teacher’s score predictions as fixed
supervision signals rather than learnable components.

**中文:**

相比之下，单位矩阵近似——在辅助模型与教师模型处停止梯度——提供了更干净、更直接的监督信号。该近似与分数蒸馏方法背后的动机一致：教师模型充当提供监督信号的固定评估器，而不是学生训练目标中的可微组件。通过把由教师与辅助模型算出的平均流视为固定目标，我们避免了因沿复杂且可能病态的神经 ODE 反向传播而造成的梯度畸变。这种务实的近似策略在蒸馏文献中已有先例。分数蒸馏采样（Score Distillation Sampling，SDS）(Poole et al., 2023) 及其变体同样停止通过教师扩散模型的梯度，把教师的分数预测视为固定监督信号而非可学习组件。

---

<span id="S020-04"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-04

**Original:**

We do not claim a tight theoretical bound on the error introduced by omitting the Jacobian term. Deriving such a bound
for neural ODE distillation remains an open problem shared by SDS, VSD, DMD, and DMD2-style objectives, which
also treat teacher predictions as fixed guidance targets. Importantly, the approximation affects the optimization trajectory
rather than the fixed point characterized by Theorem 4.3: if the expected average velocity fields are matched exactly, the
Mean Flow Matching Theorem still implies P=Q . The approximation is therefore best understood as an empirically
necessary optimization device for reaching a stable solution in high-dimensional models, not as a change to the distributional
alignment condition itself.

**中文:**

我们并不声称给出了省略 Jacobian 项所引入误差的严格理论界。为神经 ODE 蒸馏导出这样的界仍是一个开放问题，SDS、VSD、DMD 与 DMD2 类目标也共享这一局限——它们同样把教师预测作为固定引导目标。重要的是，该近似影响的是优化轨迹，而不是定理 4.3 所刻画的固定点：若期望平均速度场被精确匹配，Mean Flow Matching 定理仍蕴含 $P=Q$。因此，该近似最好被理解为在高维模型中达到稳定解所需的、经验上必要的优化手段，而非对分布对齐条件本身的改变。

---

<span id="S020-05"></span>
**Source:** p.20 S020-05

**Original:**

In summary, while the identity matrix approximation of the Jacobian term is mathematically inexact, it is a principled and
empirically validated engineering choice that prevents gradient distortion, stabilizes optimization, and enables MFD to
achieve state-of-the-art distillation performance. The stark directional divergence observed in Figure 3 provides compelling
evidence that omitting the Jacobian computation is not merely a computational convenience, but a necessary design decision
for robust flow matching distillation.

**中文:**

总之，虽然对 Jacobian 项的单位矩阵近似在数学上并不精确，但它是一种有原则且经经验验证的工程选择：它防止梯度畸变、稳定优化，并使 MFD 达到最先进的蒸馏性能。图 3 中观察到的方向显著偏离提供了有力证据：省略 Jacobian 计算不只是计算上的便利，而是稳健流匹配蒸馏的必要设计决策。

---

<span id="S021-13"></span>

Proof:We provide a proof by contradiction to demonstrate that minimizing the Mean Flow Distillation loss implies matching
the instantaneous velocity fields.

**证明：** 我们给出一个反证法证明，说明最小化 Mean Flow Distillation 损失蕴含匹配瞬时速度场。

<span id="S021-14"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S021-14

**Original:**

Assumption for Contradiction:Assume that the student instantaneous velocity field vP does not match the teacher
instantaneous velocity field uQ somewhere. That is, there exists a set Ω with positive measure under the measure induced by
the flow, such that for all(x, s)∈Ω:

**中文:**

**反证假设：** 假设学生的瞬时速度场 $v_P$ 在某处与教师瞬时速度场 $u_Q$ 不匹配。即存在一个在流诱导测度下具有正测度的集合 $\Omega$，使得对所有 $(x,s)\in\Omega$：

---

<span id="S021-15"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S021-15

**Original:**

vP (x, s)̸=u Q(x, s)(38)

**中文:**

$$v_P(x,s) \neq u_Q(x,s) \tag{38}$$

---

<span id="S021-16"></span>
**Source:** p.21 S021-16

**Original:**

Given that the vector fields satisfy Lipschitz continuity conditions, if they differ at a point, they must differ in a local
neighborhood. Thus, there exists a continuous region (a ball of radius ϵ >0 ) around some point (x∗, s∗)∈Ω where the
inequality holds strictly. Let us denote this region asB ϵ.

**中文:**

由于向量场满足 Lipschitz 连续性条件，若它们在某点不同，则必在某个局部邻域内不同。因此，在某个点 $(x^*,s^*)\in\Omega$ 周围存在连续区域（半径 $\epsilon>0$ 的球），其中不等式严格成立。记该区域为 $B_\epsilon$。

If vP (x, τ)̸=u Q(x, τ) consistently within the measurable region Bϵ, for any starting time s inside this region, there exists
a sufficiently smallδ >0such that for allt∈(s, s+δ], the integral averages (the mean flows) must also differ:

直观地，两个在某点处严格分离的连续函数，不可能在该点附近的任意小闭区间上具有相同的平均值。

这意味着 $L_{MFD}>0$，直接与 $L_{MFD}=0$ 的前提矛盾。

<span id="S022-07"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-07

**Original:**

C.3. MFD Has Lower Variance

**中文:**

### C.3 MFD 具有更低的方差

---

<span id="S022-08"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-08

**Original:**

In this part, through theoretical analysis, we demonstrate that Mean Flow Distillation (MFD) exhibits significantly lower
gradient estimation variance than Variational Score Distillation (VSD), thereby substantially stabilizing the training process.

**中文:**

本部分通过理论分析证明：Mean Flow Distillation（MFD）的梯度估计方差显著低于变分分数蒸馏（Variational Score Distillation，VSD），从而大幅稳定训练过程。

---

<span id="S022-09"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-09

**Original:**

Assuming that the neural network’s estimation of the true vector field is unbiased and subject to stochastic errors, which
may arise from limited model capacity, mini-batch sampling, parameter approximation errors, or stochastic regularization
such as dropout. Let v(Xt, t) denote the true instantaneous velocity field. The neural network estimation ˆv(Xt, t) can be
modeled as the sum of the true field and a zero-mean noise term:

**中文:**

假设神经网络对真实向量场的估计是无偏的，但带有随机误差；这些误差可能来自模型容量受限、小批量采样、参数近似误差，或 dropout 之类的随机正则化。设 $v(X_t,t)$ 表示真实瞬时速度场，则神经网络估计 $\hat v(X_t,t)$ 可建模为真实场与零均值噪声项之和：

---

<span id="S022-10"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-10

**Original:**

ˆv(Xt, t) =v(X t, t) +ξ(X t, t),withE[ξ] = 0, ,Cov[ξ(X t, t)] = Σ(Xt, t),(44)

**中文:**

$$\hat v(X_t,t) = v(X_t,t) + \xi(X_t,t),\quad \text{其中 } \mathbb{E}[\xi]=0,\ \ \mathrm{Cov}\left[\xi(X_t,t)\right] = \Sigma(X_t,t), \tag{44}$$

> 原抽取文本中“withE[ξ] = 0, ,Cov[…]”有两个逗号且 with 与前式粘连，此处按语义整理。

---

<span id="S022-11"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-11

**Original:**

here Σ(Xt, t) is the covariance matrix of the noise. For simplicity, we assume that the noise has an isotropic constant
variance σ2I over short time intervals. The gradient in Variational Score Distillation (VSD) (Wang et al., 2023) depends
directly on the difference of instantaneous scores or instantaneous velocities. Consequently, the VSD gradient estimator
gVSD is proportional to the instantaneous estimation error:

**中文:**

这里 $\Sigma(X_t,t)$ 是噪声的协方差矩阵。为简化，我们假设在短时间区间上噪声具有各向同性常数方差 $\sigma^2 I$。变分分数蒸馏（VSD）(Wang et al., 2023) 中的梯度直接依赖瞬时分数或瞬时速度之差，因此 VSD 梯度估计器 $g_{VSD}$ 正比于瞬时估计误差：

---

<span id="S022-12"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-12

**Original:**

gV SD ∝ˆv(Xt, t)−ˆuQ(Xt, t)(45)

**中文:**

$$g_{VSD} \propto \hat v(X_t,t) - \hat u_Q(X_t,t) \tag{45}$$

---

<span id="S022-13"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-13

**Original:**

The variance is therefore directly inherited from the noise in the instantaneous estimation.

**中文:**

因此其方差直接继承自瞬时估计中的噪声。

---

<span id="S022-14"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-14

**Original:**

Var(gV SD )∝Var(v(X t, t))−Var(u Q(Xt, t))≈2σ 2 (46)

**中文:**

$$\mathrm{Var}(g_{VSD}) \propto \mathrm{Var}\left(v(X_t,t)\right) - \mathrm{Var}\left(u_Q(X_t,t)\right) \approx 2\sigma^2 \tag{46}$$

> 式 (46) 中两个方差项之间的符号原文即为减号，此处照录。

---

<span id="S022-15"></span>
**Source:** p.22 S022-15

**Original:**

This implies that the gradient variance of VSD is directly bounded by the noise level σ2 of a single forward pass, and cannot
be reduced through a single-step computation.

**中文:**

这意味着 VSD 的梯度方差直接受单次前向传播噪声水平 $\sigma^2$ 的限制，无法通过单步计算降低。

---

<span id="S023-05"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-05

**Original:**

This result reveals a critical insight: when K= 1 (single-step ODE solver), MFD degenerates to a form similar to VSD with
comparable variance (Var(gM F D)≈Var(g V SD )), as the temporal integration provides no averaging effect. The variance
reduction benefit of MFD emerges only when K >1 , where the temporal integration mechanism explicitly averages
estimation noise across multiple time steps, yielding the 1/K variance scaling. In our experiments, we use a fixed ODE
solver step size∆τ(e.g., 0.02 for 4D occupancy and 0.03 for text-to-image), and the effective number of integration steps
Kvaries adaptively with each sampled time interval:K≈(t−s)/∆τ.

**中文:**

这一结果揭示了一个关键洞见：当 $K=1$（单步 ODE 求解器）时，MFD 退化为类似 VSD 的形式，方差相当（$\mathrm{Var}(g_{MFD})\approx\mathrm{Var}(g_{VSD})$），因为时间积分不提供平均效应。只有当 $K>1$ 时，MFD 的方差降低优势才显现：时间积分机制在多个时间步上显式平均估计噪声，得到 $1/K$ 的方差缩放。在我们的实验中，我们使用固定的 ODE 求解器步长 $\Delta\tau$（例如 4D 占据预测取 0.02，文本到图像生成取 0.03），有效积分步数 $K$ 随每个采样时间区间自适应变化：$K\approx(t-s)/\Delta\tau$。

---

<span id="S023-06"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-06

**Original:**

C.4. Necessity of the Auxiliary Flow Model

**中文:**

### C.4 辅助流模型的必要性

---

<span id="S023-07"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-07

**Original:**

In this section, we demonstrate both empirically and theoretically that the auxiliary flow model vP is essential for Mean
Flow Distillation. We show that attempting to replace the marginal velocity field vP with conditional velocity fields leads to
training failure and theoretical inconsistency with Theorem 4.1.

**中文:**

本节从经验与理论两方面说明辅助流模型 $v_P$ 对 Mean Flow Distillation 是必不可少的。我们表明，若试图用条件速度场替代边缘速度场 $v_P$，会导致训练失败以及与定理 4.1 的理论不一致。

---

<span id="S023-08"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-08

**Original:**

C.4.1. EMPIRICALEVIDENCE: TRAININGCOLLAPSEWITHOUTAUXILIARYMODEL

**中文:**

#### C.4.1 经验证据：缺少辅助模型时的训练崩溃

---

<span id="S023-09"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-09

**Original:**

We conducted ablation experiments on the text-to-image generation task (SANA 1.6B (Xie et al., 2025)) where we
attempted to train without the auxiliary flow model by directly using conditional velocity fields. Instead of using an
auxiliary model vP to approximate the marginal velocity field of the student distribution P , we directly use the conditional
velocity field vP (Xt|X1) where X1 =G θ(X0) is the student’s generated sample. Under the linear interpolation path
Xt = (1−t)X 0+tX1, this conditional velocity is simplyvP (Xt|X1) =X 1 −X0. This variant failed to produce meaningful
results. The training led to rapid collapse within the first 500 iterations. The generated images quickly degenerated into
blurry, semantically meaningless outputs with extremely low Image Reward (Xu et al., 2023) (<0.0) and CLIP Scores (Hessel
et al., 2021) (<20.0).

**中文:**

我们在文本到图像生成任务（SANA 1.6B (Xie et al., 2025)）上进行了消融实验，尝试不使用辅助流模型、直接使用条件速度场进行训练。我们不使用辅助模型 $v_P$ 来近似学生分布 $P$ 的边缘速度场，而是直接使用条件速度场 $v_P(X_t|X_1)$，其中 $X_1 = G_\theta(X_0)$ 是学生生成的样本。在线性插值路径 $X_t = (1-t)X_0 + tX_1$ 下，该条件速度就是 $v_P(X_t|X_1) = X_1 - X_0$。该变体未能产生有意义的结果。训练在前 500 次迭代内迅速崩溃。生成的图像很快退化为模糊、语义无意义的输出，Image Reward (Xu et al., 2023) 极低（<0.0），CLIP 分数 (Hessel et al., 2021) 也极低（<20.0）。

---

<span id="S023-10"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-10

**Original:**

These empirical failures motivated us to rigorously investigate the theoretical foundations of why the auxiliary model is
necessary.

**中文:**

这些经验失败促使我们严格考察辅助模型为何必要这一理论基础。

---

<span id="S023-11"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-11

**Original:**

C.4.2. THEORETICALANALYSIS: CONDITIONAL VS. MARGINALVELOCITYFIELDS

**中文:**

#### C.4.2 理论分析：条件速度场与边缘速度场

---

<span id="S023-12"></span>
**Source:** p.23 S023-12

**Original:**

We now prove that replacing the marginal velocity field vP with conditional velocity fields violates the distributional
equivalence stated in Theorem 4.1, leading to degenerate solutions. Let P denote the student distribution induced by Gθ,
whereX 1 =G θ(X0)withX 0 ∼ N(0, I). Consider the linear interpolation pathX t = (1−t)X 0 +tX 1. The conditional
velocity field along this path is:

**中文:**

我们现在证明，用条件速度场替代边缘速度场 $v_P$ 会违反定理 4.1 所述的分布等价性，从而导致退化解。设 $P$ 表示由 $G_\theta$ 诱导的学生分布，其中 $X_1 = G_\theta(X_0)$、$X_0 \sim \mathcal{N}(0,I)$。考虑线性插值路径 $X_t = (1-t)X_0 + tX_1$。该路径上的条件速度场为：

vP (Xt|X1) =X 1 −X 0 = dXt

(51)

**中文:**

$$v_P(X_t|X_1) = X_1 - X_0 = \left.\frac{dX_t}{dt}\right|_{X_1\ \text{fixed}} \tag{51}$$

**Source:** p.23 S023-14

**Original:**

The marginal velocity fieldv P (Xt, t)(learned by the auxiliary model) is defined as the conditional expectation:

**中文:**

辅助模型所学到的边缘速度场 $v_P(X_t,t)$ 定义为条件期望：

vP (Xt, t) =E[X 1 −X 0|Xt] =E[v P (Xt|X1)|Xt]\(52)

$$v_P(X_t,t) = \mathbb{E}[X_1 - X_0 | X_t] = \mathbb{E}\left[v_P(X_t|X_1)\big|X_t\right] \tag{52}$$

These two velocity fields are fundamentally different. The conditional velocity vP (Xt|X1) depends on the specific sample
X1, while the marginal velocityv P (Xt, t)averages over all possibleX 1 values that could have led to the observedX t.

这两个速度场本质上不同。条件速度 $v_P(X_t|X_1)$ 依赖具体的样本 $X_1$，而边缘速度 $v_P(X_t,t)$ 对可能产生观测到 $X_t$ 的所有可能 $X_1$ 取值进行平均。

<span id="S024-06"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-06

**Original:**

The conditional velocity field vP (Xt|X1) =X 1 −X 0 is defined along a specific interpolation path between X0 and a
fixed endpoint X1 =G θ(X0). For any given Xt, there are multiple possible (X0, X1) pairs that could have generated this
intermediate point through linear interpolation. Specifically, for any Xt and time t∈(0,1) , the set of possible endpoints is:

**中文:**

条件速度场 $v_P(X_t|X_1) = X_1 - X_0$ 是沿 $X_0$ 与固定端点 $X_1 = G_\theta(X_0)$ 之间特定插值路径定义的。对任意给定的 $X_t$，可能有多个 $(X_0,X_1)$ 对通过线性插值生成该中间点。具体地，对任意 $X_t$ 与时间 $t\in(0,1)$，可能的端点集合为：

---

<span id="S024-07"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-07

**Original:**

X1(Xt, t) ={X 1 :X t = (1−t)X 0 +tX 1 for someX 0 ∼p 0}(55)

**中文:**

$$X_1(X_t,t) = \left\{X_1 : X_t = (1-t)X_0 + tX_1 \text{ 对某个 } X_0\sim p_0\right\} \tag{55}$$

---

<span id="S024-08"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-08

**Original:**

Each different (X0, X1) pair gives a different conditional velocity vP (Xt|X1) =X 1 −X 0. However, a valid marginal
velocity field vP (Xt, t) as required by Theorem 4.1 must be a function that assigns a unique velocity vector to each (Xt, t)
pair. The conditional velocity vP (Xt|X1) is multi-valued at each Xt, and thus cannot directly serve as a marginal velocity
field in the sense of Theorem 4.1.

**中文:**

每个不同的 $(X_0,X_1)$ 对给出不同的条件速度 $v_P(X_t|X_1) = X_1 - X_0$。然而，定理 4.1 所要求的有效边缘速度场 $v_P(X_t,t)$ 必须是为每个 $(X_t,t)$ 对赋予唯一速度向量的函数。条件速度 $v_P(X_t|X_1)$ 在每个 $X_t$ 处是多值的，因此不能直接充当定理 4.1 意义上的边缘速度场。

---

<span id="S024-09"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-09

**Original:**

The proper marginal velocity field should be:

**中文:**

正确的边缘速度场应为：

---

<span id="S024-10"></span>
**Source:** p.24 S024-10

**Original:**

vP (Xt, t) =E[X 1 −X 0|Xt] =E[v P (Xt|X1)|Xt]\(56)

**中文:**

$$v_P(X_t,t) = \mathbb{E}[X_1 - X_0|X_t] = \mathbb{E}\left[v_P(X_t|X_1)\big|X_t\right] \tag{56}$$

---

However, since the optimization is also over the choice of Gθ and the expectation is taken over X0 ∼p 0, the solution that

然而，由于优化还涉及 $G_\theta$ 的选择，且期望是对 $X_0\sim p_0$ 取的，使期望损失最小的解是 $G_\theta$ 变得与 $X_0$ 无关：

G∗
θ(X0)≈E X0,t[X0 +u Q(Xt, t)]≈E X∼Q[X] = ¯X(63)

$$G_\theta^*(X_0) \approx \mathbb{E}_{X_0,t}\left[X_0 + u_Q(X_t,t)\right] \approx \mathbb{E}_{X\sim Q}[X] = \bar X \tag{63}$$

<span id="S025-06"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-06

**Original:**

This is a deterministic generator that maps all inputs to the same constant output, resulting in a degenerate student distribution
P=δ ¯X, which clearly violatesP=QunlessQitself is a point mass.

**中文:**

这是一个确定性生成器，它把所有输入映射到同一常数输出，导致退化的学生分布 $P=\delta_{\bar X}$；除非 $Q$ 本身是点质量，否则这显然违反 $P=Q$。

---

<span id="S025-07"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-07

**Original:**

When using the auxiliary model vP (Xt, t) =E[X 1 −X 0|Xt], we obtain a proper marginal velocity field that assigns a
unique velocity vector to each (Xt, t) pair. This marginal velocity field can satisfy the instantaneous velocity field matching
condition in Theorem 4.1:

**中文:**

当使用辅助模型 $v_P(X_t,t) = \mathbb{E}[X_1 - X_0|X_t]$ 时，我们得到一个为每个 $(X_t,t)$ 对赋予唯一速度向量的有效边缘速度场。该边缘速度场可以满足定理 4.1 中的瞬时速度场匹配条件：

---

<span id="S025-08"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-08

**Original:**

vP (Xt, t) =u Q(Xt, t)for all(X t, t)(64)

**中文:**

$$v_P(X_t,t) = u_Q(X_t,t)\quad \text{对所有 } (X_t,t) \tag{64}$$

---

<span id="S025-09"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-09

**Original:**

By Theorem 4.1, this equality of instantaneous marginal velocity fields guaranteesP=Q , achieving the correct distributional
matching. The auxiliary model learns this marginal field by training on samples from the evolving student distribution,
effectively computing the conditional expectationE[X 1 −X 0|Xt]through regression.

**中文:**

由定理 4.1，瞬时边缘速度场的这一相等性保证 $P=Q$，从而实现正确的分布匹配。辅助模型通过在被更新的学生分布样本上训练来学习该边缘场，实际上是通过回归计算条件期望 $\mathbb{E}[X_1 - X_0|X_t]$。

---

<span id="S025-10"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-10

**Original:**

Conclusion.

**中文:**

**结论。**

---

<span id="S025-11"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-11

**Original:**

The conditional velocity field vP (Xt|X1) is fundamentally inadequate for distillation because it is not a valid marginal
velocity field as required by Theorem 4.1. It is multi-valued at each Xt (depending on which specific X1 generated that
point) and cannot represent a proper flow. The auxiliary flow model vP (Xt, t), which learns the marginal velocity field
through conditional expectation, is not merely a technical convenience but a theoretical necessity for achieving distributional
equivalence as stated in Theorem 4.1. The empirical training failures observed in our experiments directly confirm this
mathematical conclusion.

**中文:**

条件速度场 $v_P(X_t|X_1)$ 对蒸馏而言本质上是不充分的，因为它不是定理 4.1 所要求的有效边缘速度场。它在每个 $X_t$ 处是多值的（取决于哪个具体的 $X_1$ 生成了该点），无法表示一个正常的流。辅助流模型 $v_P(X_t,t)$ 通过条件期望学习边缘速度场，它不仅是技术上的便利，而是实现定理 4.1 所述分布等价性的理论必要条件。我们实验中观察到的经验训练失败直接证实了这一数学结论。

---

**Source:** p.25 S025-12

**Original:**

D. Additional Experiment Results

**中文:**

## D 附加实验结果

---

<span id="S025-13"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-13

**Original:**

D.1. CIFAR-10 Distribution Coverage Analysis

**中文:**

### D.1 CIFAR-10 分布覆盖分析

---

<span id="S025-14"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-14

**Original:**

To directly evaluate whether MFD preserves distributional coverage rather than only improving fidelity, we conduct an
additional unconditional CIFAR-10 (Krizhevsky et al., 2009) experiment. All single-step methods and the teacher use the
same approximately 42M-parameter UNet backbone under comparable training settings. We report FID, precision, and
recall, where recall is the primary coverage-oriented metric for assessing possible mode dropping.

**中文:**

为直接评估 MFD 是否保持分布覆盖而不只是提升保真度，我们补充了一个无条件 CIFAR-10 (Krizhevsky et al., 2009) 实验。所有单步方法与教师使用同一约 42M 参数的 UNet 主干，训练设置相当。我们报告 FID、precision 与 recall，其中 recall 是评估潜在模式丢失的主要覆盖导向指标。

---

<span id="S025-15"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-15

**Original:**

Table 6 shows that MFD achieves the best FID and recall among all single-step methods while maintaining high precision.
The recall results are particularly informative: DMD2 retains 66.3% of the teacher’s recall (0.173 vs. 0.261) and Diff-Instruct
retains 72.8% (0.190 vs. 0.261), whereas MFD retains 81.2% (0.212 vs. 0.261). MFD also improves over IMM and
MeanFlow, which are trained from scratch rather than distilled from the teacher. This indicates that MFD mitigates the
recall degradation commonly associated with distillation.

**中文:**

表 6 显示 MFD 在所有单步方法中取得最佳 FID 与 recall，同时保持较高的 precision。recall 结果尤其有信息量：DMD2 保留教师 recall 的 66.3%（0.173 vs. 0.261），Diff-Instruct 保留 72.8%（0.190 vs. 0.261），而 MFD 保留 81.2%（0.212 vs. 0.261）。MFD 还优于 IMM 与 MeanFlow，后者是从零训练而非从教师蒸馏。这表明 MFD 缓解了蒸馏常见的 recall 退化。

---

<span id="S025-16"></span>
**Source:** p.25 S025-16

**Original:**

These results are consistent with the theoretical objective of MFD. Score-distillation methods align single-timestep scores or
velocities, whose training signal can be weak in low-density regions. MFD supervises the student through mean velocities
over full time intervals and, under the assumptions of Theorem 4.3, converges to P=Q when the expected average velocity

**中文:**

这些结果与 MFD 的理论目标一致。分数蒸馏方法对齐单时间步的分数或速度，其训练信号在低密度区域可能较弱。MFD 通过整个时间区间上的平均速度来监督学生，并且在定理 4.3 的假设下，当期望平均速度

<span id="S026-01"></span>

**Original:**

fields match. Since MFD is a distillation method, its diversity is bounded by the teacher distribution; the relevant empirical
question is therefore how much coverage the student retains relative to the teacher. The CIFAR-10 recall results show that
MFD retains substantially more teacher coverage than other single-step distillation baselines.

<span id="S026-02"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-02

**Original:**

D.2. Toy Example of Mean Flow Distillation

**中文:**

### D.2 Mean Flow Distillation 的玩具示例

---

<span id="S026-03"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-03

**Original:**

To provide concrete visual evidence for the variance reduction property of MFD, we conduct a controlled experiment on a
2D toy example where we can directly visualize and compare the gradient vector fields produced by VSD and MFD under
identical noise conditions. The experiment is designed to isolate and quantify the temporal filtering effect that distinguishes
mean flow matching from instantaneous velocity matching.

**中文:**

为给 MFD 的方差降低性质提供具体的可视化证据，我们在一个 2D 玩具示例上进行了受控实验：在相同噪声条件下，可直接可视化并比较 VSD 与 MFD 产生的梯度向量场。该实验旨在分离并量化区分平均流匹配与瞬时速度匹配的时间滤波效应。

---

<span id="S026-04"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-04

**Original:**

We construct a simple setup where a student distribution P is being aligned with a teacher distribution Q in a 2D space. To
ensure a fair comparison, both VSD and MFD experiments are initialized from the same pretrained flow matching model
and use this identical model as the teacher uQ, differing only in their supervision strategies. For VSD, we compute the
instantaneous velocity field discrepancy at a fixed time pointt= 0.5 : ∆V SD =v P (Xt, t)−u Q(Xt, t), which represents
the supervision signal that would be used to update the student model. For MFD, we compute the average velocity field
discrepancy over a time interval [s, t]: ∆M F D = ˆU P (Xs, s, t)− ˆU Q(Xs, s, t), where the mean velocities ˆU are obtained
through ODE integration withK= 12steps.

**中文:**

我们构造了一个简单设置：在 2D 空间中把学生分布 $P$ 与教师分布 $Q$ 对齐。为保证公平比较，VSD 与 MFD 实验都从同一个预训练流匹配模型初始化，并使用该相同模型作为教师 $u_Q$，仅在监督策略上不同。对 VSD，我们计算固定时间点 $t=0.5$ 处的瞬时速度场差异 $\Delta_{VSD} = v_P(X_t,t) - u_Q(X_t,t)$，它代表用于更新学生模型的监督信号。对 MFD，我们计算时间区间 $[s,t]$ 上的平均速度场差异 $\Delta_{MFD} = \hat U_P(X_s,s,t) - \hat U_Q(X_s,s,t)$，其中平均速度 $\hat U$ 通过 $K=12$ 步的 ODE 积分得到。

---

<span id="S026-05"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-05

**Original:**

To simulate the optimization noise inherent in real training scenarios, we model the auxiliary model’s velocity output as
ˆv=v+ξ , where ξ∼ N(0, σ 2) represents independent Gaussian noise at each evaluation point. Crucially, in the VSD
case, this noise appears directly in the gradient signal at the single sampled time point, fully preserving the high-frequency
fluctuations. In the MFD case, however, we inject independent noise ξk at each of the K integration steps along the
trajectory. According to the law of large numbers, these zero-mean independent noise terms partially cancel out during the
temporal integration, resulting in an effective variance reduction by a factor of1/K as proven in Section C.3. To ensure
fair comparison, we normalize the noise injection such that both methods experience equivalent total noise energy, and we
further normalize the resulting vector fields by their respective average magnitudes to compare directional stability rather
than absolute scale.

**中文:**

为模拟真实训练场景中固有的优化噪声，我们把辅助模型的速度输出建模为 $\hat v = v + \xi$，其中 $\xi\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^2)$ 表示每个评估点上的独立高斯噪声。关键在于：在 VSD 情形下，该噪声直接出现在单个采样时间点的梯度信号中，完整保留高频波动；而在 MFD 情形下，我们在轨迹的 $K$ 个积分步的每一步注入独立噪声 $\xi_k$。根据大数定律，这些零均值独立噪声项在时间积分过程中部分相互抵消，从而产生如 C.3 节所证明的 $1/K$ 量级的有效方差降低。为保证公平比较，我们对噪声注入进行归一化，使两种方法经历等效的总噪声能量；并进一步用各自平均幅值对所得向量场归一化，以比较方向稳定性而非绝对尺度。

---

<span id="S026-06"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-06

**Original:**

The visualization in Figure 4 presents the resulting gradient vector fields side by side. The VSD field exhibits chaotic, wildly
varying directions across the spatial domain—the vectors appear to “explode” in random directions due to the unfiltered
noise at each evaluation point. In stark contrast, the MFD field maintains smooth, coherent flow structure despite being
subjected to noise of equivalent total energy. The vectors in the MFD case consistently point in similar directions within
local neighborhoods, demonstrating that the temporal integration successfully acts as a low-pass filter that suppresses
high-frequency noise while preserving the underlying transport direction. This visual comparison directly validates our
theoretical claim that MFD functions as a temporal smoothing mechanism, transforming noisy instantaneous supervision into
stable trajectory-level guidance. The quantitative analysis further confirms that the gradient variance of MFD scales inversely
with the number of integration steps K, providing both intuitive and rigorous evidence for why mean flow distillation
achieves superior training stability compared to instantaneous velocity matching approaches.

**中文:**

图 4 的可视化并排展示了所得梯度向量场。VSD 场在整个空间域呈现混乱、剧烈变化的方向——由于每个评估点处未经过滤的噪声，向量似乎沿随机方向“爆炸”。形成鲜明对比的是，MFD 场尽管承受了总能量等效的噪声，仍保持平滑、连贯的流结构。MFD 情形下的向量在局部邻域内始终指向相近方向，表明时间积分成功起到低通滤波器的作用：抑制高频噪声，同时保留底层传输方向。这一可视化比较直接验证了我们的理论主张——MFD 起到时间平滑机制的作用，把含噪的瞬时监督转化为稳定的轨迹级引导。定量分析进一步确认，MFD 的梯度方差与积分步数 $K$ 成反比，为“平均流蒸馏为何比瞬时速度匹配获得更优训练稳定性”提供了既直观又严格的证据。

---

<span id="S026-07"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-07

**Original:**

D.3. Qualitative Results on 4D Occupancy Forecasting

**中文:**

### D.3 4D 占据预测的定性结果

---

<span id="S026-08"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-08

**Original:**

Figure 5 presents a qualitative comparison of 4D occupancy forecasting results on the nuScenes dataset. When the teacher
model OccFM is forced to perform single-step sampling (NFE=1), it produces severely degraded predictions with missing
structures and noisy artifacts, as shown in column (c). In contrast, the student model distilled by MFD with the same
single-step inference (column b) preserves fine-grained semantic details and spatial structures that are highly comparable to
the ground truth (column a). This visual comparison demonstrates the effectiveness of mean flow distillation in maintaining
generation quality under extreme acceleration, corroborating the quantitative improvements reported in the main text.

**中文:**

图 5 给出了 nuScenes 数据集上 4D 占据预测结果的定性比较。当教师模型 OccFM 被迫执行单步采样（NFE=1）时，它产生的预测严重退化，结构缺失并带有噪声伪影，如列 (c) 所示。相比之下，由 MFD 蒸馏得到的学生模型在相同单步推理下（列 b）保留了与真值（列 a）高度可比的细粒度语义细节与空间结构。这一可视化比较表明平均流蒸馏在极端加速下仍能保持生成质量，印证了正文报告的定量提升。

---

<span id="S026-09"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-09

**Original:**

D.4. Ablation Study of ODE Step Size on Text-to-Image Generation

**中文:**

### D.4 文本到图像生成上 ODE 步长的消融研究

---

<span id="S026-10"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-10

**Original:**

The ablation study on ODE solver step size on text-to-image generation is shown in Table 7.

**中文:**

文本到图像生成中 ODE 求解器步长的消融研究见表 7。

---

<span id="S026-11"></span>
**Source:** p.26 S026-11

**Original:**

Moderate step sizes (0.005 and 0.03) achieve strong performance, with 0.03 yielding the best results across most metrics.
This optimal step size corresponds to approximately 30-35 integration steps, which strikes an effective balance between
capturing the mean flow trajectory accurately and providing robust gradient signals through natural averaging of noisy
velocity predictions.

**中文:**

中等步长（0.005 与 0.03）取得强劲性能，其中 0.03 在多数指标上给出最佳结果。该最优步长对应约 30–35 个积分步，在准确捕捉平均流轨迹与通过自然平均含噪速度预测提供稳健梯度信号之间取得了有效平衡。

<span id="S027-03"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S027-03

**Original:**

Larger step sizes (0.1 and 0.5) show mixed results. Interestingly, step size 0.1 achieves the highest Aesthetic Score (6.546)
and competitive CLIP Score (29.441), suggesting that for certain perceptual qualities, a coarser mean flow approximation
with 10 integration steps may be sufficient. However, the ImageReward metric drops to 0.8264 at 0.1 and further degrades
to 0.7582 at 0.5, indicating that extremely coarse approximations fail to capture fine-grained semantic alignment with human
preferences. At step size 0.5 (only 2 integration steps), the mean flow computation approaches a single-step approximation
similar to VSD, forfeiting the variance reduction benefits and resulting in degraded performance across most metrics.

**中文:**

较大步长（0.1 与 0.5）表现参差。有趣的是，步长 0.1 取得最高 Aesthetic Score（6.546）与有竞争力的 CLIP Score（29.441），说明对某些感知质量而言，仅 10 个积分步的较粗平均流近似可能已足够。然而 ImageReward 在 0.1 时降至 0.8264，并在 0.5 时进一步降至 0.7582，表明极粗糙的近似无法捕捉与人类偏好一致的细粒度语义对齐。在步长 0.5（仅 2 个积分步）时，平均流计算接近单步近似、与 VSD 类似，放弃了方差降低收益，导致多数指标性能下降。

---

<span id="S027-04"></span>
**Source:** p.27 S027-04

**Original:**

The optimal step size of 0.03 represents a sweet spot where the variance reduction is substantial enough to stabilize training,
while avoiding excessive numerical errors or over-smoothing that could harm generation quality. This consistency across
both 4D occupancy forecasting and text-to-image generation demonstrates that MFD’s performance is robust to the choice
of integration granularity, with a wide range of moderate step sizes (0.01-0.05) yielding near-optimal results.

**中文:**

最优步长 0.03 代表一个最佳平衡点：方差降低足够显著以稳定训练，同时避免可能损害生成质量的过度数值误差或过度平滑。这一结论在 4D 占据预测与文本到图像生成上的一致性表明，MFD 的性能对积分粒度的选择具有鲁棒性，较宽范围的中等步长（0.01–0.05）都能给出接近最优的结果。

<span id="S028-03"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S028-03

**Original:**

D.6. Mean Flow Distance Evolution Analysis

**中文:**

### D.6 平均流距离演化分析

---

<span id="S028-04"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S028-04

**Original:**

To further understand the training dynamics of MFD, we analyze the evolution of the L2 distance between the teacher mean
flow ˆU Q and the auxiliary mean flow ˆU P throughout the distillation process. Figure 7 visualizes this distance (in log scale)
as a function of training steps during text-to-image generation experiments.

**中文:**

为进一步理解 MFD 的训练动力学，我们分析蒸馏过程中教师平均流 $\hat U_Q$ 与辅助平均流 $\hat U_P$ 之间 L2 距离的演化。图 7 把该距离（对数尺度）可视化为文本到图像生成实验中训练步数的函数。

---

<span id="S028-05"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S028-05

**Original:**

The trajectory reveals three distinct phases of the distillation process.Phase 1 (Early Training):During the initial training
phase, the L2 distance between mean flows rapidly increases. This counter-intuitive behavior has a clear explanation: both
the auxiliary model vϕ and student generator Gθ are initialized from the pretrained teacher model weights. At initialization,
the auxiliary model accurately represents the teacher distribution Q, hence the mean flows are nearly identical. However, as
training begins, the auxiliary model vϕ requires time to adapt and learn to approximate this new, evolving student distribution
P . During this adaptation period, the auxiliary mean flow temporarily diverges from the teacher mean flow, causing the
observed increase in distance.

**中文:**

该轨迹揭示了蒸馏过程的三个不同阶段。**阶段 1（训练早期）：** 在初始训练阶段，平均流之间的 L2 距离迅速增大。这一反直觉行为有清楚的解释：辅助模型 $v_\phi$ 与学生生成器 $G_\theta$ 都由预训练教师模型的权重初始化。初始化时，辅助模型准确表示教师分布 $Q$，因此平均流几乎相同。然而随着训练开始，辅助模型 $v_\phi$ 需要时间适应并学习近似这个新的、不断演化的学生分布 $P$。在这一适应期内，辅助平均流暂时偏离教师平均流，造成观察到的距离增大。

---

<span id="S028-06"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S028-06

**Original:**

Phase 2 (Adaptation):After the initial divergence, the distance stabilizes and begins a gradual descent. This indicates
that the auxiliary model successfully captures the student distribution and the overall system starts to align the student’s
distribution P with the teacher’s distributionQ through mean flow matching. The MFD objective effectively guides both
models toward distributional equivalence.

**中文:**

**阶段 2（适应）：** 在初始偏离之后，距离趋于稳定并开始逐渐下降。这表明辅助模型成功捕捉到学生分布，整个系统开始通过平均流匹配使学生分布 $P$ 与教师分布 $Q$ 对齐。MFD 目标有效地把两个模型引向分布等价。

---

<span id="S028-07"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S028-07

**Original:**

Phase 3 (Sustained Training):Notably, the distance does not rapidly converge to zero but instead maintains a gradual,
slow declining trend. This behavior is fundamentally different from standard supervised learning scenarios and reflects
the unique co-evolutionary dynamics of distillation. Unlike traditional training where the target is fixed, in MFD both the
student model Gθ and the auxiliary model vϕ are continuously updated. Each update to Gθ changes the student distribution
P , which in turn requires vϕ to adapt. This creates a moving target scenario: the auxiliary model must track a continuously
evolving distribution rather than converge to a static target. The sustained distance reflects this ongoing adaptation process,
where the auxiliary model constantly adjusts to match the current student distribution while the student simultaneously
moves toward the teacher distribution.

**中文:**

**阶段 3（持续训练）：** 值得注意的是，距离并不会迅速收敛到零，而是保持缓慢、逐渐下降的趋势。这一行为与标准监督学习情形根本不同，反映了蒸馏特有的协同演化动力学。与传统训练中目标固定不同，在 MFD 中学生模型 $G_\theta$ 与辅助模型 $v_\phi$ 都在持续更新。对 $G_\theta$ 的每次更新都会改变学生分布 $P$，进而要求 $v_\phi$ 适应。这形成移动目标的情形：辅助模型必须跟踪持续演化的分布，而不是收敛到静态目标。持续存在的距离反映了这一持续适应过程——辅助模型不断调整以匹配当前学生分布，而学生同时向教师分布移动。

---

<span id="S028-08"></span>
**Source:** p.28 S028-08

**Original:**

This analysis provides crucial insight into the convergence behavior of MFD. The gradual distance reduction, rather than

**中文:**

这一分析为 MFD 的收敛行为提供了关键洞见。距离的逐渐下降（而非

> 本段在 PDF 第 28 页末尚未结束，续接第 29 页首段（“……迅速坍缩）表明训练动力学是健康的”）。

eventually trends downward confirms that the distillation objective successfully encourages alignment between student and
teacher distributions, despite the complexity introduced by the co-evolving auxiliary model. This empirical observation
validates the theoretical framework of MFD and demonstrates that the auxiliary flow model can effectively track the student
distribution even as it continuously changes during training.

**中文:**

迅速坍缩）表明训练动力学是健康的：两个模型在协同学习。距离最终呈下降趋势这一事实确认：尽管协同演化的辅助模型引入了复杂性，蒸馏目标仍成功促进学生分布与教师分布的对齐。这一经验观察验证了 MFD 的理论框架，并表明辅助流模型即使在训练中持续变化时也能有效跟踪学生分布。

D.7. The Generation Samples of Text-to-Image Generation

### D.7 文本到图像生成的生成样本

---

<span id="S029-05"></span>

**Original:**

To further demonstrate the visual quality and diversity of MFD-distilled models, we present qualitative generation results

[第 29 页](#page-29)

<span id="page-30"></span>
## PDF 第 30 页

<span id="S030-01"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-01

**Original:**

E. Ethical Statement

**中文:**

## E 伦理声明

---

<span id="S030-02"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-02

**Original:**

The potential ethical impact of our work is about fairness. As “human” is included as a kind of object in the LiDAR scene,
when performing scene completion, it may be necessary to complete human figures. Human-related objects may exhibit
data bias related to fairness issues, such as biases in gender or skin color. Such bias can be captured by the student model in
the training.

**中文:**

我们工作的潜在伦理影响与公平性有关。由于“人”在 LiDAR 场景中会作为一种对象出现，在执行场景补全时可能需要补全人体。与人相关的对象可能表现出与公平性问题相关的数据偏差，例如在性别或肤色上的偏差。这种偏差可能在训练中被学生模型捕捉。

---

<span id="S030-03"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-03

**Original:**

E.1. Notification to human subjects

**中文:**

### E.1 对人类受试者的通知

---

<span id="S030-04"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-04

**Original:**

This work focuses on improving the efficiency of generative and predictive models through distillation and does not introduce
new data sources or deployment mechanisms.

**中文:**

本工作聚焦于通过蒸馏提升生成与预测模型的效率，并不引入新的数据来源或部署机制。

---

<span id="S030-05"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-05

**Original:**

In the autonomous driving experiments, our model operates on LiDAR-based 4D occupancy representations, which
encode anonymous spatial occupancy and motion patterns without containing personally identifiable information or visual
attributes of individuals. As such, the proposed method does not involve identity recognition, demographic inference, or
decision-making about human attributes.

**中文:**

在自动驾驶实验中，我们的模型运行于基于 LiDAR 的 4D 占据表示，它编码匿名的空间占据与运动模式，不包含个人可识别信息或个体的视觉属性。因此，所提方法不涉及身份识别、人口统计推断或关于人类属性的决策。

---

<span id="S030-06"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-06

**Original:**

In the text-to-image generation experiments, the model is trained and evaluated on publicly available datasets and generates
generic image content rather than representations of real individuals.

**中文:**

在文本到图像生成实验中，模型在公开可用的数据集上训练与评估，生成通用图像内容，而非真实个体的再现。

---

<span id="S030-07"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-07

**Original:**

While the proposed method may contribute to applications in safety-critical systems such as autonomous driving, it is
intended as a research tool for improving model efficiency. Responsible deployment, system-level validation, and adherence
to safety regulations remain the responsibility of downstream application developers.

**中文:**

虽然所提方法可能有助于自动驾驶等安全关键系统中的应用，但它旨在作为提升模型效率的研究工具。负责任的部署、系统级验证以及对安全法规的遵守，仍由下游应用开发者负责。

---

<span id="S030-08"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-08

**Original:**

F. Limitation

**中文:**

## F 局限性

---

<span id="S030-09"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-09

**Original:**

While Mean Flow Distillation demonstrates strong performance and improved training stability across several challenging
tasks, this work still has some limitations. First, MFD relies on numerical ODE integration to estimate the mean velocity
fields of both the teacher and auxiliary models. Although this integration is performed over relatively short intervals and
uses a small number of steps, it inevitably introduces approximation errors that depend on the choice of solver and step size.
Our ablations show that moderate step sizes are robust, but practitioners may still need a small sweep when transferring
MFD to new domains. More adaptive or solver-aware integration strategies could further improve robustness.

**中文:**

虽然 Mean Flow Distillation 在若干具有挑战性的任务上表现出强劲性能并提升了训练稳定性，本工作仍存在一些局限。第一，MFD 依赖数值 ODE 积分来估计教师与辅助模型的平均速度场。尽管该积分在相对较短的区间上、以少量步数执行，它不可避免地引入依赖求解器与步长选择的近似误差。我们的消融表明中等步长是鲁棒的，但实践者在新领域迁移 MFD 时可能仍需要做小范围扫描。更自适应或求解器感知的积分策略可进一步提升鲁棒性。

---

<span id="S030-10"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-10

**Original:**

Second, MFD introduces an auxiliary flow model to approximate the student-induced distribution, which increases training
complexity and computational overhead compared to purely single-network distillation approaches. The auxiliary model is
training-only and can be implemented with lightweight adapters in large-scale settings, but reducing its training cost remains
an important direction for future work.

**中文:**

第二，MFD 引入辅助流模型来近似学生诱导的分布，这相比纯单网络蒸馏方法增加了训练复杂度与计算开销。辅助模型仅用于训练，在大规模场景中可用轻量适配器实现，但降低其训练成本仍是未来工作的重要方向。

---

<span id="S030-11"></span>
**Source:** p.30 S030-11

**Original:**

Finally, our theoretical analysis assumes Lipschitz continuity of the velocity fields and sufficient capacity of the auxiliary
model to approximate the student distribution. In addition, the practical optimization omits the Jacobian term through a
fixed-guidance approximation; while empirically necessary and consistent with prior distillation practice, a tight theoretical
bound on this approximation remains open. Exploring sharper theoretical guarantees and broader empirical evaluations is
left to future work.

**中文:**

最后，我们的理论分析假设速度场满足 Lipschitz 连续性，并假设辅助模型具有足够容量来近似学生分布。此外，实际优化通过固定引导近似省略了 Jacobian 项；尽管这在经验上是必要的且与先前蒸馏实践一致，该近似的严格理论界仍是开放问题。更锐利的理论保证与更广泛的实证评估留待未来工作。

---

## 术语表

| English | 中文 | 用法说明 |
|---|---|---|
| Flow Matching (FM) | 流匹配 | 学习把初始分布映射到目标分布的确定性 ODE 速度场的生成范式 |
| Mean Flow Distillation (MFD) | 均值流蒸馏 | 本文提出的蒸馏框架，以时间积分的平均速度场作为对齐目标 |
| mean flow / average velocity field (AVF) | 均值流 / 平均速度场 | 速度场在区间 [s,t] 上的时间平均，充当时间低通滤波器 |
| instantaneous velocity field matching | 瞬时速度场匹配 | 定理 4.1：逐点匹配速度场即足以保证分布匹配 |
| auxiliary flow model | 辅助流模型 | 训练时拟合学生分布 P 瞬时速度场的 v_P，推理时丢弃 |
| student model / one-step generator | 学生模型 / 单步生成器 | G_θ：把噪声直接映射为样本的单步生成器 |
| variational score distillation (VSD) | 变分分数蒸馏 | 以教师分数为先验、用辅助扩散模型近似学生分数的蒸馏方法，梯度方差高 |
| distillation | 蒸馏 | 把多步迭代采样的教师模型压缩为少步或单步学生模型 |
| NFE (number of function evaluations) | 函数评估次数 | 推理中网络前向调用次数，越小表示采样越快 |
| Bregman divergence | Bregman 散度 | 定理 4.1/4.3 中度量两个速度场差异的严格正定散度 |
| temporal low-pass filter | 时间低通滤波器 | 对速度场做时间积分以抑制高频优化噪声的机制 |
| TTUR | 双时间尺度更新规则 | 辅助模型与学生模型按 10:1 交替更新的训练策略 |
| LoRA | 低秩适配 | 仅训练低秩增量参数的高效微调方式，本文秩 r=64 |
| 4D occupancy forecasting | 4D 占据预测 | 自动驾驶中预测未来时空体占据状态的感知任务 |
| CFG (classifier-free guidance) | 无分类器引导 | 以引导尺度 w 放大条件与无条件速度场差异的采样技巧 |
| average velocity field (AVF) | 平均速度场 | 区间 [s,t] 上瞬时速度的时间积分平均，U(X_s,s,t) = (1/(t-s))∫_s^t u_Q(ψ_τ(X_0),τ)dτ（式 28） |
| auxiliary flow model v_P | 辅助流模型 | 训练期用于重建学生诱导分布边缘速度场的变分代理模型，推理时丢弃（附录 B.2.3、C.4） |
| marginal velocity field | 边缘速度场 | 对可能生成 X_t 的所有端点取条件期望得到的唯一速度场（式 52、56） |
| conditional velocity field | 条件速度场 | 给定具体端点 X_1 的速度场 v_P(X_t\|X_1)=X_1−X_0；命题 C.1 证明其无法充当边缘速度场 |
| Lipschitz continuity | Lipschitz 连续性 | 定理 4.1/4.3 与附录 F 假设速度场满足的连续性条件 |
| temporal boxcar filter | 时间矩形窗滤波 | 平均值算子 (1/Δt)∫(·)dτ 的时域名称，频域幅度响应为 \|sinc(fΔt)\|，起低通滤波作用（式 29） |
| variance reduction | 方差降低 | MFD 的核心性质：梯度估计方差按 Var(g_MFD) ∝ (1/K)Var(g_VSD) 缩放（式 49–50、C.3 节） |
| Jacobian term / identity matrix approximation | Jacobian 项 / 单位矩阵近似 | 对辅助与教师模型停止梯度、把 Jacobian 视为单位阵的近似（附录 B.6） |
| NFE | 函数评估次数 | number of function evaluations；单步方法 NFE=1，教师 NFE=100 |
| precision / recall (distribution coverage) | 精确率 / 召回率（分布覆盖） | CIFAR-10 实验中评估分布覆盖的指标，recall 用于衡量模式丢失（表 6、D.1 节） |
| toy example | 玩具示例 | 2D 受控实验，用于可视化并比较 VSD 与 MFD 的梯度向量场（图 4、D.2 节） |
